--- 🔄 Strategy Iteration 1/5 ---
--- Strategy Report Received ---
**1. Problem Statement**

Refinement iteration of the 2D inviscid compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor instability (single mode, effective gravity in $+y$, heavy fluid below light) on $[0,0.25]\times[0,1.0]$, $t\in[0,3.0]$. The previous DGSEM run scored 97/100 and the Advisor mandated three targeted fixes: (i) audit the timestep diagnostic against the raw accepted-step history, (ii) recompute the symmetry residual on a correctly paired, cell-centered grid, and (iii) perform the `polydeg=6, CFL=0.30` resolution check. Strategy: retain the validated DGSEM architecture unchanged in its physics components, promote the resolution-check configuration to production, and rebuild the two flawed diagnostics.

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D compressible Euler with gravitational source:
$$
\partial_t \begin{pmatrix}\rho\\ \rho u\\ \rho v\\ E\end{pmatrix}
+ \partial_x \begin{pmatrix}\rho u\\ \rho u^2 + p\\ \rho u v\\ u(E+p)\end{pmatrix}
+ \partial_y \begin{pmatrix}\rho v\\ \rho u v\\ \rho v^2 + p\\ v(E+p)\end{pmatrix}
= \begin{pmatrix}0\\ 0\\ \rho g\\ \rho g v\end{pmatrix},
\qquad
p = (\gamma-1)\left(E - \tfrac{1}{2}\rho(u^2+v^2)\right)
$$
* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$, $t \in [0, 3.0]$
* **Boundary Conditions:** $x$: Periodic ($x=0 \equiv x=0.25$). $y=0$ and $y=1$: Reflective / free-slip walls (no-penetration $v=0$).
* **Initial Conditions:**
$$
\rho(x,y,0) = 2.0 + (1.0-2.0)\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}\left[1 + \tanh\!\left(50(y-0.5)\right)\right]
$$
$$
p(y,0) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5\\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5\end{cases}
\qquad
u(x,y,0) = 0
$$
$$
v(x,y,0) = -0.025\, c(x,y)\, \cos(8\pi x)\, \sin^6(\pi y), \qquad c(x,y) = \sqrt{\gamma\, p(y,0)/\rho(x,y,0)}
$$
$$
E(x,y,0) = \frac{p(y,0)}{\gamma-1} + \tfrac{1}{2}\rho(x,y,0)\, v(x,y,0)^2
$$
* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; $g = 1.0$ (source adds momentum in $+y$: $\mathbf{S} = (0,0,\rho g, \rho g v)^T$); $\rho_{heavy}=2.0$ (bottom), $\rho_{light}=1.0$ (top); interface $y_{int}=0.5$; smoothing $50$; perturbation amplitude $0.025$; single mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ ($\lambda = 0.25$, exactly one period across the domain).
* **Expected Physics:** RTI growth with heavy spike rising at $x=0.125$ and light bubbles descending at $x=0,\,0.25$: single-mode displacement by $t\approx0.75$; spike/bubble development $t\approx1.5$; mushroom with KH roll-up $t\approx2.25$; secondary KH vortices and interaction with the top wall by $t=3.0$. Pressure remains near hydrostatic with late-time compression near $y=1$. The continuous solution is exactly mirror-symmetric about $x=0.125$ for all $t$ (exact property used by the symmetry diagnostic).

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Exact solution feasibility: NO.** The full nonlinear 2D system with a gravitational source, solid walls, and a seeded single-mode perturbation has no closed-form solution. The Method of Characteristics yields exact implicit solutions only for 1D simple-wave/Riemann problems; in 2D the characteristic fields do not decouple into tractable invariants, and the late-time dynamics (mushroom roll-up, secondary KH, wall interaction) are inherently multi-scale. Linearized analysis describes only the early exponential growth phase. The sole exact solution of the system is the unperturbed hydrostatic equilibrium ($dp/dy = \rho g$), which holds only approximately at the smoothed interface and is not the requested evolution. A numerical method is therefore mandatory.
* **Method selection: DGSEM in 2D plus time.** Fourier spectral is ruled out by the non-periodic $y$-walls. DGSEM provides high order for the smooth vortex roll-ups, p4est-based local AMR to track the interface, and indicator-based shock capturing for late-time compressions — precisely the architecture the Advisor endorsed ("For Optimality: None").
* **Resolution decision (Advisor-mandated check):** `polydeg=6, CFL=0.30`, retaining HLLC, the pressure-based HG indicator, and the current AMR level limits. This run **is** the Advisor's single non-repeating resolution check, and the higher-degree result is adopted as production: RTI continuously generates finer scales, the open question is convergence of the $t=3$ vortex cores, and higher order with tighter CFL is the conservative, feature-resolving choice. The comparison baseline is the documented `polydeg=5` Run 3 (min $\rho = 0.35441$, min $p = 1.20991$, mass drift $4.9\times10^{-12}$).
* **Sensor selection:** AMR on density; shock capturing on pressure (justification in 3.0.1).

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** Use template defaults for all unlisted settings. The following Advisor-mandated overrides supersede all defaults.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing — Variable Selection Analysis:**
  * *AMR sensor: density $\rho$.* The structural features (material interface, spike, bubbles, mushroom rims) are all defined by $\rho$, which stays sharp and localized at these features and bounded ($\sim[0.3, 2.2]$) — never globally chaotic. Velocity is rejected: after KH onset ($t\gtrsim 2$) it becomes broadband-turbulent over the whole mixing zone, so refining on it wastes elements everywhere. Pressure is rejected as an AMR sensor: it is dominated by the smooth hydrostatic gradient and is nearly uniform along the interface. Density is strictly superior for this PDE.
  * *Shock-capturing sensor: pressure.* It must detect compressions and wall-interaction shocks (where entropy is produced), not the contact interface; a density-based indicator would deposit dissipation directly on the RTI feature of interest. This dual-sensor assignment is retained from the validated runs per the Advisor.

* **3.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides):**
  1. **Resolution check as production:** `polydeg=6`, `CFL=0.30` (via StepsizeCallback), retaining HLLC surface flux, pressure-based IndicatorHennemannGassner shock capturing (flux_ranocha DG / flux_lax_friedrichs FV), density-based IndicatorLöhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel (base=4, med=5, max=6), interval=20, `adapt_initial_condition=true`, RDPK3SpFSAL49, `adaptive=false`, `tspan=(0.0, 3.0)`, and the validated base mesh. Sample $\rho$ and $p$ onto a **fixed, documented cell-centered uniform grid of $512 \times 2048$ points** at $t=2.25$ and $t=3.0$ and save them (e.g., `.npz`). Print a labeled `RESOLUTION CHECK` block: min/max $\rho$, min $p$, mixing-zone area ($1.1 < \rho < 1.9$), mean $|\nabla\rho|$ inside the mixing zone, and a vortex-core count proxy — side-by-side with Run 3's documented polydeg=5 values. Declare the change **material** if mixing/sharpness metrics shift by more than ~10% relative or new resolved sub-structures appear.
  2. **Timestep audit (Integrity):** Record the raw accepted-step history $(t_n, \mathrm{dt}_n)$ for **every** step. Report in a labeled `TIMESTEP AUDIT` block: (a) min/median/max dt over **ordinary integration steps only**; (b) the final (possibly `tspan`-clipped) step's dt separately; (c) the initialization value separately; (d) the count of steps with $\mathrm{dt} < 10^{-8}$ and whether their times coincide with AMR adaptation events. This determines whether $\mathrm{dt}_{min} = 5.55\times10^{-17}$ was a final-step clipping artifact (expected) or a recurring AMR/StepsizeCallback pathology. CFL=0.30 is adopted as part of the check configuration, not blindly.
  3. **Symmetry residual fix (Accuracy):** Compute residuals on a **cell-centered** uniform $x$-grid, $x_i = (i+\tfrac{1}{2})\Delta x$, $\Delta x = 0.25/512$, **without a duplicated periodic endpoint**, using the exact mirror pairing $i \leftrightarrow N_x - 1 - i$ (reflection about $x = 0.125$ under periodicity). Report $R_q = \|q - Mq\|_2 / \|q\|_2$ **separately for each conservative variable** $q \in \{\rho, \rho u, \rho v, \rho E\}$ at $t = 0, 0.75, 1.5, 2.25, 3.0$ in a labeled `SYMMETRY MONITOR` block, to identify when asymmetry first grows. The $t=0$ residual must be at machine precision — this validates the diagnostic itself.
  4. **Prohibitions (Advisor):** Do NOT switch to HLL/Lax–Friedrichs surface fluxes; do NOT add indiscriminate stabilization; no ringing is demonstrated and contacts must remain sharp.
  5. **Retained diagnostics:** mass drift vs $t=0$ (Trixi.integrate), positivity (min $\rho$, min $p$), wall no-penetration (max $|v|$ at $y=0$ and $y=1$), and the $t=0$ renderer $x$-independence test (max mirrored deviation of rendered $\rho$, $p$) — all printed at every save.

**4. Visualization Strategy**

* **Field Selection:** $\rho$ (primary — interface, spike, bubble, and mushroom dynamics are the RTI drivers) and $p$ (secondary — hydrostatic stratification plus late-time wall compression). Do not plot $u, v, E$.
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ (top row) and $p$ (bottom row) at $t = 0.75, 1.5, 2.25, 3.0$ — a $2 \times 4$ grid.
* **Layout/Style:** Sample the DG solution onto the uniform Cartesian grid (no element striping, no mesh lines). **CRITICAL: disable colorbars (`colorbar=false`) — Julia.** Per-panel automatic color scaling; no fixed color ranges.
* **Saving Data:** Save the **full solution state** (all variables) at `dt_save = 0.1` (plus initial/final) for Ground Truth; additionally save the fixed-grid $\rho, p$ arrays at $t=2.25, 3.0$ per §3.1.1.
* **Save as:** `summary_all.png` (strictly).

**5. Cross-Validation & Data Integration**

* No external reference data (sensors / true data) are provided in the problem statement → external Relative $L_2$ comparison is **not applicable**.
* Internal verification suite replaces ground truth: conservation (mass drift), positivity, wall no-penetration, $t=0$ machine-precision symmetry, per-variable symmetry residuals vs. time, the timestep audit, and the polydeg=5 → polydeg=6 resolution metrics against Run 3's documented values.

**REQUIRED COMPONENTS**

**Main Method:**
Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time

**Enhancements:**
* Shock Capturing Indicator (pressure-based Hennemann–Gassner, hybrid DG–FV)
* Adaptive Mesh Refinement (density-based Löhner, three-level controller 4/5/6)
* Advisor-mandated resolution-check configuration (polydeg=6, CFL=0.30) — adopted as production
* Accepted-step timestep-history audit (Integrity diagnostic)
* Mirror-indexed, per-variable, multi-time symmetry-residual diagnostic (Accuracy diagnostic)

--- Critic report---
REJECTED

**Critique:** The plan unconditionally promotes `polydeg=6, CFL=0.30` to production, contradicting the Advisor’s instruction to do so **only if** the resolution check materially improves the interface or vortex features. Moreover, Run 3’s documented scalar diagnostics do not provide the identical-grid mixing-zone and vortex-core baseline needed for the promised comparison.

--- Strategy Report Received ---
**1. Problem Statement**

Refinement iteration of the 2D inviscid compressible Euler Rayleigh–Taylor instability (single mode, effective gravity in $+y$, heavy fluid below light) on $[0,0.25]\times[0,1.0]$, $t\in[0,3.0]$. Run 3 scored 97/100 with a validated DGSEM architecture. The Advisor mandated: (i) a timestep audit against the raw accepted-step history, (ii) a corrected symmetry diagnostic, and (iii) **one non-repeating resolution check** at `polydeg=6, CFL=0.30` — with promotion **conditional on material improvement**. The Critic rejected the previous plan's unconditional promotion. Corrected strategy: the production configuration **remains Run 3's `polydeg=5, CFL=0.35`** by default; the higher-degree run is executed **solely as the resolution check**; the polydeg=5 baseline is **regenerated on the identical sampling grid** (Run 3's archived scalars lack the required structural metrics); and a production selection is made only through an explicit, printed decision gate.

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D compressible Euler with gravitational source:
$$
\partial_t \begin{pmatrix}\rho\\ \rho u\\ \rho v\\ E\end{pmatrix}
+ \partial_x \begin{pmatrix}\rho u\\ \rho u^2 + p\\ \rho u v\\ u(E+p)\end{pmatrix}
+ \partial_y \begin{pmatrix}\rho v\\ \rho u v\\ \rho v^2 + p\\ v(E+p)\end{pmatrix}
= \begin{pmatrix}0\\ 0\\ \rho g\\ \rho g v\end{pmatrix},
\qquad
p = (\gamma-1)\left(E - \tfrac{1}{2}\rho(u^2+v^2)\right)
$$
* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$, $t \in [0, 3.0]$
* **Boundary Conditions:** $x$: Periodic ($x=0 \equiv x=0.25$). $y=0$ and $y=1$: Reflective / free-slip walls (no-penetration $v=0$).
* **Initial Conditions:**
$$
\rho(x,y,0) = 2.0 + (1.0-2.0)\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}\left[1 + \tanh\!\left(50(y-0.5)\right)\right]
$$
$$
p(y,0) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5\\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5\end{cases}
\qquad
u(x,y,0) = 0
$$
$$
v(x,y,0) = -0.025\, c(x,y)\, \cos(8\pi x)\, \sin^6(\pi y), \qquad c(x,y) = \sqrt{\gamma\, p(y,0)/\rho(x,y,0)}
$$
$$
E(x,y,0) = \frac{p(y,0)}{\gamma-1} + \tfrac{1}{2}\rho(x,y,0)\, v(x,y,0)^2
$$
* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; $g = 1.0$ (source adds momentum in $+y$: $\mathbf{S} = (0,0,\rho g, \rho g v)^T$); $\rho_{heavy}=2.0$ (bottom), $\rho_{light}=1.0$ (top); interface $y_{int}=0.5$; smoothing $50$; perturbation amplitude $0.025$; single mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ ($\lambda = 0.25$, exactly one period across the domain).
* **Expected Physics:** RTI growth with the heavy-fluid spike rising in $+y$ at $x=0.125$ and light-fluid bubbles descending at $x=0,\,0.25$: single-mode displacement by $t\approx0.75$; spike/bubble development $t\approx1.5$; mushroom with KH roll-up $t\approx2.25$; secondary KH vortices and interaction with the top wall by $t=3.0$. Pressure remains near hydrostatic with late-time compression near $y=1$. The continuous solution is exactly mirror-symmetric about $x=0.125$ for all $t$ (exact property exploited by the symmetry diagnostic).

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Exact solution feasibility: NO.** The full nonlinear 2D system with gravitational source, solid walls, and a seeded single-mode perturbation has no closed-form solution. The Method of Characteristics yields exact implicit solutions only for 1D simple-wave/Riemann problems; in 2D the characteristic fields do not decouple, and the late-time dynamics (mushroom roll-up, secondary KH, wall interaction) are inherently multi-scale. Linearized analysis covers only early exponential growth. The Advisor explicitly confirmed this ("no feasible closed-form analytical solution"). A numerical method is mandatory.
* **Method selection: DGSEM in 2D plus time.** Fourier spectral is ruled out by the non-periodic $y$-walls. DGSEM provides high order for the smooth roll-ups, p4est AMR to track the interface, and indicator-based shock capturing for late-time compressions — the architecture the Advisor endorsed ("For Optimality: None").
* **Two-stage, decision-gated design (Critic-corrected):**
  * **Stage A — Production baseline (default):** Run 3's exact physics configuration (`polydeg=5`, `CFL=0.35`, HLLC, pressure-based HG shock capturing, density-based Löhner AMR 4/5/6, RDPK3SpFSAL49, `adaptive=false`), re-executed with the corrected diagnostic suite. Because Run 3's archived output contains only scalar diagnostics, Stage A **regenerates the polydeg=5 baseline** — fixed-grid $\rho, p$ samples and structural metrics on the identical sampling grid.
  * **Stage B — Resolution check (non-repeating):** `polydeg=6`, `CFL=0.30`, **everything else identical** (fluxes, indicators, AMR limits, integrator, mesh, tspan). Executed solely as the Advisor's resolution check.
  * **Decision gate:** Both stages are compared on the identical cell-centered grid at $t=2.25$ and $t=3.0$ against explicit materiality criteria. The polydeg=6 configuration is promoted **only** upon demonstrated material improvement; otherwise Run 3's polydeg=5 configuration is retained as production. The verdict is printed. Both stages render their panels during their own run, so the final `summary_all.png` is selected from already-computed data — no third run is required for either outcome.

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** Use template defaults for all unlisted settings. The overrides below supersede all defaults.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing — Variable Selection Analysis:**
  * *AMR sensor: density $\rho$.* The structural features (material interface, spike, bubbles, mushroom rims) are all defined by $\rho$, which stays sharp, localized, and bounded ($\sim[0.3, 2.2]$) — never globally chaotic. Velocity is rejected: after KH onset ($t\gtrsim 2$) it becomes broadband over the whole mixing zone, wasting elements everywhere. Pressure is rejected as AMR sensor: it is dominated by the smooth hydrostatic gradient and nearly uniform along the interface. Density is strictly superior for this PDE.
  * *Shock-capturing sensor: pressure.* It must detect compressions and wall-interaction shocks (where entropy is produced), not the contact interface; a density-based indicator would deposit dissipation directly on the RTI feature of interest. Dual-sensor assignment retained per the Advisor.

* **3.1 Advisor/Critic-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides):**
  1. **Shared configuration (both stages):** P4estMesh on $[0,0.25]\times[0,1]$, periodic in $x$, slip walls at $y=0,1$; HLLC surface flux; pressure-based IndicatorHennemannGassner shock capturing (flux_ranocha DG / flux_lax_friedrichs FV); density-based IndicatorLöhner AMR, ControllerThreeLevel (base=4, med=5 @0.1, max=6 @0.3), interval=20, `adapt_initial_condition=true`; RDPK3SpFSAL49, `adaptive=false`, `tspan=(0.0, 3.0)`.
  2. **Stage A (production default):** `polydeg=5`, `CFL=0.35` — Run 3's validated configuration. Full diagnostic suite (items 4–6, 8). Save full state at `dt_save=0.1`; save fixed-grid $\rho, p$ samples at $t=2.25, 3.0$; render and store its $2\times4$ panel figure.
  3. **Stage B (resolution check only):** `polydeg=6`, `CFL=0.30` — identical in all other respects. Same diagnostic suite; same fixed-grid samples at $t=2.25, 3.0$; render and store its panel figure.
  4. **Identical-grid comparison and decision gate (Details):** Both stages sample onto the **same** cell-centered uniform grid of $512 \times 2048$ points ($\Delta x = 0.25/512$, $\Delta y = 1/2048$). Compute for each stage at $t=2.25$ and $t=3.0$: (a) mixing-zone area $A_{mix} = \#\{(x,y): 1.1 < \rho < 1.9\}$; (b) mean interfacial sharpness $S = \langle|\nabla\rho|\rangle$ over the mixing zone; (c) structural count = number of disconnected heavy-fluid pockets ($\rho > 1.9$ above $y=0.5$) plus light-fluid pockets ($\rho < 1.1$ below $y=0.5$). Print a labeled `RESOLUTION CHECK` block: metric | polydeg=5 | polydeg=6 | relative shift | material? — at both times. **Material improvement is declared if ANY of:** (M1) $A_{mix}$ shifts $>10\%$ relative at $t=3.0$; (M2) $S$ shifts $>10\%$ relative at $t=3.0$; (M3) the structural count changes or new resolved sub-structures appear in the $t=3.0$ vortex cores on the identical grid. Print the verdict explicitly: `VERDICT: MATERIAL — promoting polydeg=6, CFL=0.30 to production` **or** `VERDICT: NOT MATERIAL — retaining polydeg=5, CFL=0.35 (Run 3 configuration)`. The selected run's stored figure is written as the final `summary_all.png`. **Do not promote polydeg=6 absent a demonstrated material change.**
  5. **Timestep audit (Integrity, both stages):** Record the raw accepted-step history $(t_n, \mathrm{dt}_n)$ for **every** step. Print a labeled `TIMESTEP AUDIT` block: (a) min/median/max dt over **ordinary integration steps only**; (b) the final (possibly `tspan`-clipped) step's dt separately; (c) the initialization value separately; (d) count of steps with $\mathrm{dt} < 10^{-8}$ and whether their times coincide with AMR adaptation events (interval=20). This determines whether Run 3's $\mathrm{dt}_{min}=5.55\times10^{-17}$ was a final-step clipping artifact (expected) or a recurring AMR/StepsizeCallback pathology. CFL is changed **only** as part of the Stage B check, not blindly.
  6. **Symmetry residual fix (Accuracy, both stages):** Compute residuals on a **cell-centered** uniform $x$-grid, $x_i = (i+\tfrac{1}{2})\Delta x$, $\Delta x = 0.25/512$, **without a duplicated periodic endpoint**, using the exact mirror pairing $i \leftrightarrow N_x - 1 - i$ (reflection about $x=0.125$ under periodicity; exact since $x_{N_x-1-i} = 0.25 - x_i$). Report $R_q = \|q - Mq\|_2/\|q\|_2$ **separately for each conservative variable** $q \in \{\rho, \rho u, \rho v, \rho E\}$ at $t = 0, 0.75, 1.5, 2.25, 3.0$ in a labeled `SYMMETRY MONITOR` block. The $t=0$ residual must be at machine precision — this validates the diagnostic itself.
  7. **Symmetry contingency (conditional, per Advisor):** Only if the corrected residual for any variable still exceeds $\sim 10^{-2}$ at $t=3.0$: run a **symmetric fixed-mesh control run at comparable degrees of freedom (no AMR)** to distinguish AMR-induced symmetry breaking from physical chaotic amplification; report both residuals. Do not trigger this otherwise.
  8. **Retained diagnostics (both stages, printed at every save):** mass drift vs $t=0$ (Trixi.integrate), positivity (min $\rho$, min $p$), wall no-penetration (max $|v|$ at $y=0$ and $y=1$), and the $t=0$ renderer $x$-independence test (max mirrored deviation of rendered $\rho$, $p$).
  9. **Prohibitions (Advisor):** Do NOT switch to HLL/Lax–Friedrichs surface fluxes; do NOT add indiscriminate stabilization; do NOT change the AMR level limits; no ringing is demonstrated and contacts must remain sharp.

**4. Visualization Strategy**

* **Field Selection:** $\rho$ (primary — interface, spike, bubble, and mushroom dynamics are the RTI drivers) and $p$ (secondary — hydrostatic stratification plus late-time wall compression). Do not plot $u, v, E$.
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ (top row) and $p$ (bottom row) at $t = 0.75, 1.5, 2.25, 3.0$ — a $2 \times 4$ grid.
* **Layout/Style:** Sample the DG solution onto the uniform Cartesian grid (no element striping, no mesh lines). **CRITICAL: disable colorbars (`colorbar=false`) — Julia.** Per-panel automatic color scaling; no fixed color ranges.
* **Saving Data:** Save the **full solution state** (all variables) at `dt_save = 0.1` (plus initial/final) for Ground Truth in the production run; save the fixed-grid $\rho, p$ arrays at $t=2.25, 3.0$ for **both** stages (comparison input).
* **Production selection:** Both stages render and store their own $2\times4$ figures during execution; after the decision gate (§3.1.4), the selected production run's figure is written as `summary_all.png`. **Save as:** `summary_all.png` (strictly).

**5. Cross-Validation & Data Integration**

* No external reference data (sensors / true data) are provided → external Relative $L_2$ comparison is **not applicable** (Advisor-confirmed: no ground truth exists for this forward nonlinear RTI run).
* Internal verification suite replaces ground truth: conservation (mass drift), positivity, wall no-penetration, machine-precision $t=0$ symmetry, per-variable symmetry residuals vs. time, the accepted-step timestep audit, and the **quantitative polydeg=5 vs. polydeg=6 identical-grid comparison** (mixing-zone area, interfacial sharpness, structural counts) — the latter serving as the convergence evidence for the smallest $t=3$ vortices.

Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time

```julia
@inline function pressure_indicator(u, equations::CompressibleEulerEquations2D)
    rho, rho_v1, rho_v2, rho_e = u
    return (equations.gamma - 1) *
           (rho_e - 0.5 * (rho_v1^2 + rho_v2^2) / rho)
end

function rti_build_dg(polydeg)
    basis = LobattoLegendreBasis(polydeg)
    indicator_sc = IndicatorHennemannGassner(equations, basis,
                                             alpha_max=1.0,
                                             alpha_min=0.001,
                                             alpha_smooth=true,
                                             variable=pressure_indicator)
    volume_integral = VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG(indicator_sc;
                                                     volume_flux_dg=flux_ranocha,
                                                     volume_flux_fv=flux_lax_friedrichs)
    return DGSEM(polydeg=polydeg, surface_flux=flux_hllc,
                 volume_integral=volume_integral)
end

# Stage A production default; Stage B reuses rti_build_dg with polydeg=6.
polydeg = 5
dg = rti_build_dg(polydeg)
```

```julia
rti_amr_interval = 20

function rti_build_amr(semi_stage)
    indicator = IndicatorLöhner(semi_stage, variable=density_indicator)
    controller = ControllerThreeLevel(semi_stage, indicator,
                                      base_level=4,
                                      med_level=5, med_threshold=0.1,
                                      max_level=6, max_threshold=0.3)
    return AMRCallback(semi_stage, controller,
                       interval=rti_amr_interval,
                       adapt_initial_condition=true)
end

# Used by the raw accepted-step recorder to tag scheduled AMR interventions.
@inline function rti_amr_event_due(integrator)
    return integrator.iter > 0 &&
           integrator.iter % rti_amr_interval == 0
end

# Stage A default; Stage B rebuilds these objects from its own semidiscretization.
amr_callback = rti_build_amr(semi)
```

```julia
# Each run owns its history and output state. In particular, Stage B must not
# overwrite Stage A's accepted steps, snapshots, or symmetry residuals.
mutable struct RTIStageDiagnostics
    label::String
    polydeg::Int
    npz_dir::String
    initial_mass::Float64
    initialization_t::Float64
    initialization_dt::Float64
    accepted_steps::Vector{NamedTuple}
    amr_events::Vector{NamedTuple}
    snapshot_files::Dict{Float64,String}
    symmetry_residuals::Dict{Float64,Any}
    fixed_grid_samples::Dict{Float64,Any}
    final_diagnostics::Any
end

function rti_new_stage_diagnostics(label::AbstractString, degree::Integer,
                                   directory::AbstractString)
    mkpath(directory)
    return RTIStageDiagnostics(String(label), Int(degree), String(directory),
                               NaN, NaN, NaN, NamedTuple[], NamedTuple[],
                               Dict{Float64,String}(),
                               Dict{Float64,Any}(),
                               Dict{Float64,Any}(), nothing)
end

rti_default_diagnostics =
    rti_new_stage_diagnostics("stage_a_polydeg5", polydeg, npz_out)
rti_final_diagnostics = Ref{Any}(nothing)

@inline function rti_mass_integrand(u, args...)
    return u[1]
end

function rti_total_mass(u_ode, semi_local)
    mesh_local, equations_local, solver, cache =
        Trixi.mesh_equations_solver_cache(semi_local)
    u = Trixi.wrap_array(u_ode, mesh_local, equations_local, solver, cache)
    # Use DG quadrature and mesh metric terms, rather than a nodal sum.
    return Trixi.integrate(rti_mass_integrand, u, mesh_local, equations_local,
                           solver, cache)
end

function rti_initialize_dt!(integrator, state, t)
    state.initialization_t = t
    state.initialization_dt = integrator.dt
    return nothing
end

function rti_record_dt!(integrator, state; amr_enabled=true)
    # Initialization is not an accepted integration step.
    if integrator.iter == 0 && integrator.t == integrator.tprev
        if !isfinite(state.initialization_dt)
            rti_initialize_dt!(integrator, state, integrator.t)
        end
        return nothing
    end

    # The accepted dt is the time difference, so a tspan-clipped final step
    # remains visible instead of being replaced by the next proposed dt.
    dt = integrator.t - integrator.tprev
    if !isfinite(dt) || dt <= 0.0
        dt = integrator.dt
    end

    if integrator.t > 0.0 && isfinite(dt) && dt > 0.0
        amr_event = amr_enabled && rti_amr_event_due(integrator)
        push!(state.accepted_steps,
              (t=integrator.t, tprev=integrator.tprev, dt=dt,
               iter=integrator.iter, amr_event=amr_event))
        if amr_event
            push!(state.amr_events,
                  (t=integrator.t, iter=integrator.iter))
        end
    end
    return nothing
end

function rti_build_dt_callback(state; amr_enabled=true)
    affect! = integrator -> rti_record_dt!(integrator, state;
                                           amr_enabled=amr_enabled)
    initialize = function (callback, u, t, integrator)
        rti_initialize_dt!(integrator, state, t)
    end
    return DiscreteCallback((u, t, integrator) -> true, affect!;
                            initialize=initialize,
                            save_positions=(false, false))
end

function rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v, wall_y)
    distances = abs.(y .- wall_y)
    tolerance = 1.0e-10
    mask = distances .<= tolerance
    # Retain a fallback for floating-point variations in boundary coordinates.
    if !any(mask)
        mask = distances .<= minimum(distances) + tolerance
    end
    return maximum(abs.(v[mask]))
end

function rti_snapshot_file(state, target_time)
    for (t, filename) in state.snapshot_files
        if isapprox(t, target_time; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-10)
            return filename
        end
    end
    error("Missing snapshot for $(state.label) at t = $target_time")
end

function rti_save_npz_file(integrator, state)
    semi_local = integrator.p
    t = integrator.t

    # Full conservative state for Ground Truth and subsequent exact-grid
    # diagnostics. No scattered-bin symmetry residual is computed here.
    pd_cons = PlotData2D(integrator.u, semi_local;
                         solution_variables=cons2cons)
    rho = getindex.(pd_cons.data, 1)
    rho_u = getindex.(pd_cons.data, 2)
    rho_v = getindex.(pd_cons.data, 3)
    rho_e = getindex.(pd_cons.data, 4)

    pd_prim = PlotData2D(integrator.u, semi_local;
                         solution_variables=cons2prim)
    p = getindex.(pd_prim.data, 4)
    y = vec(pd_cons.y)
    v_vec = vec(getindex.(pd_prim.data, 3))

    mass = rti_total_mass(integrator.u, semi_local)
    if t == 0.0 || !isfinite(state.initial_mass)
        state.initial_mass = mass
    end
    if t == 0.0 && !isfinite(state.initialization_dt)
        state.initialization_t = t
        state.initialization_dt = integrator.dt
    end

    mass_drift = mass - state.initial_mass
    relative_mass_drift = mass_drift / state.initial_mass
    rho_min, rho_max = extrema(rho)
    p_min, p_max = extrema(p)
    wall_bottom = rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v_vec, 0.0)
    wall_top = rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v_vec, 1.0)

    dt_last = NaN
    if !isempty(state.accepted_steps)
        dt_last = state.accepted_steps[end].dt
    end

    filename = joinpath(state.npz_dir,
                        Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", t))
    npzwrite(filename, Dict("t" => t,
                            "polydeg" => state.polydeg,
                            "x" => pd_cons.x,
                            "y" => pd_cons.y,
                            "rho" => rho,
                            "rho_u" => rho_u,
                            "rho_v" => rho_v,
                            "rho_e" => rho_e,
                            "p" => p))
    state.snapshot_files[t] = filename

    state.final_diagnostics = (
        t=t,
        mass=mass,
        mass_drift=mass_drift,
        relative_mass_drift=abs(relative_mass_drift),
        rho_min=rho_min,
        rho_max=rho_max,
        p_min=p_min,
        p_max=p_max,
        dt_last=dt_last,
        initialization_dt=state.initialization_dt,
        accepted_step_count=length(state.accepted_steps),
        max_abs_v_y0=wall_bottom,
        max_abs_v_y1=wall_top
    )
    if state === rti_default_diagnostics
        rti_final_diagnostics[] = state.final_diagnostics
    end

    println("Saved: $(state.label)/$(basename(filename))")
    Printf.@printf("Diagnostics t=%.5f: mass=%.16e, mass_drift=%.3e, relative_mass_drift=%.3e\n",
                   t, mass, mass_drift, relative_mass_drift)
    Printf.@printf("  rho_min=%.6e, rho_max=%.6e, p_min=%.6e, p_max=%.6e\n",
                   rho_min, rho_max, p_min, p_max)
    Printf.@printf("  accepted_steps=%d, dt_last=%.3e, max_abs_v_y0=%.3e, max_abs_v_y1=%.3e\n",
                   length(state.accepted_steps), dt_last,
                   wall_bottom, wall_top)
    return nothing
end

# Compatibility wrapper for the Stage A default callback.
function save_npz_file(integrator)
    return rti_save_npz_file(integrator, rti_default_diagnostics)
end

function rti_build_save_callback(state)
    return PresetTimeCallback(save_times,
                              integrator -> rti_save_npz_file(integrator, state);
                              save_positions=(false, false))
end

function rti_build_io_callbacks(state; amr_enabled=true)
    return CallbackSet(rti_build_dt_callback(state;
                                             amr_enabled=amr_enabled),
                       rti_build_save_callback(state))
end

# Return statistics without discarding the raw history. The last accepted
# step is reported separately whether or not it was actually clipped.
function rti_timestep_audit(state)
    steps = state.accepted_steps
    ordinary_dts = Float64[]
    for i in 1:(length(steps) - 1)
        push!(ordinary_dts, steps[i].dt)
    end

    tiny_steps = filter(step -> step.dt < 1.0e-8, steps)
    tiny_amr_count = count(step -> step.amr_event, tiny_steps)

    return (
        accepted_step_count=length(steps),
        ordinary_step_count=length(ordinary_dts),
        ordinary_min=isempty(ordinary_dts) ? NaN : minimum(ordinary_dts),
        ordinary_median=isempty(ordinary_dts) ? NaN : median(ordinary_dts),
        ordinary_max=isempty(ordinary_dts) ? NaN : maximum(ordinary_dts),
        final_t=isempty(steps) ? NaN : steps[end].t,
        final_dt=isempty(steps) ? NaN : steps[end].dt,
        final_is_tiny=!isempty(steps) && steps[end].dt < 1.0e-8,
        initialization_t=state.initialization_t,
        initialization_dt=state.initialization_dt,
        tiny_count=length(tiny_steps),
        tiny_amr_count=tiny_amr_count,
        tiny_steps=tiny_steps,
        amr_events=state.amr_events
    )
end

# Stage A defaults; later stages use rti_build_io_callbacks with their own state.
rti_dt_callback = rti_build_dt_callback(rti_default_diagnostics)
save_npz_preset_callback = rti_build_save_callback(rti_default_diagnostics)
save_npz_callback = CallbackSet(rti_dt_callback, save_npz_preset_callback)
```

```julia
function rti_build_stage_callbacks(semi_stage, amr_stage, io_stage;
                                   cfl, output_directory)
    summary = SummaryCallback()
    analysis = AnalysisCallback(semi_stage, interval=analysis_interval)
    alive = AliveCallback(analysis_interval=analysis_interval)

    # Keep complete conservative-state output for every stage so that either
    # decision-gated production selection has restartable Ground Truth data.

        CallbackSet(summary, analysis, alive, save, io_stage, stepsize)
    else
        CallbackSet(summary, analysis, alive, save, amr_stage,
                    io_stage, stepsize)
    end

    return (callbacks=callback_set,
            summary_callback=summary,
            analysis_callback=analysis,
            alive_callback=alive,
            save_solution=save,
            stepsize_callback=stepsize)
end
```

```julia
function rti_solve_stage(ode_stage, stage_callbacks)
    solution = solve(ode_stage, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
                     dt=1.0,
                     adaptive=false,
                     callback=stage_callbacks,
                     saveat=0.01,
                     maxiters=1e7)
    isempty(solution.t) && error("Time integration returned no saved times")
    isapprox(solution.t[end], tspan[2]; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-12) ||
        error("Time integration stopped at t = $(solution.t[end]), expected $(tspan[2])")
    return solution
end

function rti_run_independent_stage(label, degree, cfl;
                                   fixed_refinement_level=nothing)
    fixed_mesh = fixed_refinement_level !== nothing

    mesh_stage = if fixed_mesh
        # A uniform P4est mesh is mirror-symmetric and receives no AMR.
        P4estMesh((1, 1), polydeg=degree,
                  coordinates_min=coordinates_min,
                  coordinates_max=coordinates_max,
                  initial_refinement_level=fixed_refinement_level,
                  periodicity=(true, false))
    else
        rti_build_mesh(degree)
    end

    dg_stage = rti_build_dg(degree)
    semi_stage = rti_build_semi(mesh_stage, dg_stage)
    amr_stage = fixed_mesh ? nothing : rti_build_amr(semi_stage)

    output_root = joinpath(@__DIR__, label)
    stage_npz_dir = joinpath(output_root, "data_npz")
    stage_trixi_dir = joinpath(output_root, "trixi_output")
    diagnostics = rti_new_stage_diagnostics(label, degree, stage_npz_dir)
    io_callbacks = rti_build_io_callbacks(diagnostics;
                                          amr_enabled=!fixed_mesh)
    callback_parts = rti_build_stage_callbacks(
        semi_stage, amr_stage, io_callbacks;
        cfl=cfl, output_directory=stage_trixi_dir)

    ode_stage = semidiscretize(semi_stage, tspan)
    println("Running $label: polydeg=$degree, CFL=$cfl, AMR=$(!fixed_mesh)")
    solution = rti_solve_stage(ode_stage, callback_parts.callbacks)

    return (
        label=label,
        polydeg=degree,
        cfl=cfl,
        semi=semi_stage,
        sol=solution,
        diagnostics=diagnostics,
        npz_dir=stage_npz_dir,
        trixi_dir=stage_trixi_dir,
        figure_file=joinpath(output_root, "summary.png"),
        fixed_refinement_level=fixed_refinement_level,
        state_dofs=length(solution.u[end])
    )
end

# Conditional diagnostic only: this function is deliberately not called here.
function rti_run_symmetric_fixed_mesh_control(reference_run)
    degree = reference_run.polydeg
    target_state_dofs = length(reference_run.sol.u[end])

    # Select the uniform level whose total conservative-state size is closest
    # to the reference run's final size. Main-run AMR limits remain unchanged.
    levels = collect(4:6)
    candidate_state_dofs = [
        4 * (2^level)^2 * (degree + 1)^2 for level in levels
    ]
    level = levels[argmin(abs.(candidate_state_dofs .- target_state_dofs))]

    label = "symmetric_fixed_control_polydeg$(degree)_level$(level)"
    println("SYMMETRY CONTINGENCY: running one fixed-mesh control without AMR")
    control = rti_run_independent_stage(label, degree, reference_run.cfl;
                                        fixed_refinement_level=level)
    Printf.@printf("Fixed-mesh control: level=%d, target_state_dofs=%d, control_state_dofs=%d\n",
                   level, target_state_dofs, control.state_dofs)
    return control
end
```

```julia
function rti_snapshot_polydeg(data, degree=nothing)
    degree !== nothing && return Int(degree)
    if haskey(data, "polydeg")
        value = data["polydeg"]
        return value isa Number ? Int(value) : Int(only(value))
    end
    return polydeg
end

# PlotData2D normally supplies three-dimensional element blocks. Also accept
# flattened serializations, using the degree belonging to the current stage.
function rti_element_blocks(array, degree::Integer)
    ndims(array) == 3 && return array
    ndims(array) == 2 ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Expected DG element-block plot data"))

    nrows, ncols = size(array)
    candidates = (degree, degree + 1, 2 * degree,
                  2 * degree + 1, 2 * (degree + 1))
    for n in candidates
        if n >= 2 && nrows == n * n
            return reshape(array, n, n, ncols)
        elseif n >= 2 && nrows == n && ncols % n == 0
            return reshape(array, n, n, div(ncols, n))
        end
    end

    throw(ArgumentError("Could not recover DG visualization element blocks"))
end
rti_element_blocks(array) = rti_element_blocks(array, polydeg)

function rti_grid_axes(nx, ny; cell_centered=false)
    if cell_centered
        dx = 0.25 / nx
        dy = 1.0 / ny
        # No duplicated periodic endpoint: x_i + x_(Nx+1-i) = 0.25.
        x_grid = [(i - 0.5) * dx for i in 1:nx]
        y_grid = [(j - 0.5) * dy for j in 1:ny]
    else
        # Retain the template's endpoint-inclusive visualization grid.
        x_grid = collect(range(0.0, 0.25, length=nx))
        y_grid = collect(range(0.0, 1.0, length=ny))
    end
    return x_grid, y_grid
end

function rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, variable;
                                 nx=256, ny=1024,
                                 degree=nothing, cell_centered=false)
    (nx > 1 && ny > 1) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Uniform grid dimensions must exceed one"))

    degree_local = rti_snapshot_polydeg(data, degree)
    x_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["x"], degree_local)
    y_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["y"], degree_local)
    z_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data[variable], degree_local)
    size(x_blocks) == size(y_blocks) == size(z_blocks) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Coordinates and solution data must have matching shapes"))

    x_grid, y_grid = rti_grid_axes(nx, ny; cell_centered=cell_centered)
    grid = fill(NaN, ny, nx)
    filled = falses(ny, nx)

    for element in axes(x_blocks, 3)
        x_block = view(x_blocks, :, :, element)
        y_block = view(y_blocks, :, :, element)
        x_min, x_max = extrema(x_block)
        y_min, y_max = extrema(y_block)

        # Determine which block dimension represents x. P4est RTI elements
        # are axis-aligned, but this also handles transposed plot data.
        x_first = view(x_blocks, :, 1, element)
        x_second = view(x_blocks, 1, :, element)
        first_spread = maximum(x_first) - minimum(x_first)
        second_spread = maximum(x_second) - minimum(x_second)

        if first_spread >= second_spread
            x_nodes = collect(x_first)
            y_nodes = collect(view(y_blocks, 1, :, element))
            z_block = Array(view(z_blocks, :, :, element))
        else
            x_nodes = collect(x_second)
            y_nodes = collect(view(y_blocks, :, 1, element))
            z_block = permutedims(Array(view(z_blocks, :, :, element)))
        end

        x_order = sortperm(x_nodes)
        y_order = sortperm(y_nodes)
        x_nodes = x_nodes[x_order]
        y_nodes = y_nodes[y_order]
        z_block = z_block[x_order, y_order]

        padding = 1.0e-12
        i_first = max(1, searchsortedfirst(x_grid, x_min - padding))
        i_last = min(nx, searchsortedlast(x_grid, x_max + padding))
        j_first = max(1, searchsortedfirst(y_grid, y_min - padding))
        j_last = min(ny, searchsortedlast(y_grid, y_max + padding))

        for j in j_first:j_last
            y_left, y_right, wy = rti_linear_bracket(y_nodes, y_grid[j])
            for i in i_first:i_last
                filled[j, i] && continue

                x_left, x_right, wx =
                    rti_linear_bracket(x_nodes, x_grid[i])

                z00 = z_block[x_left, y_left]
                z10 = z_block[x_right, y_left]
                z01 = z_block[x_left, y_right]
                z11 = z_block[x_right, y_right]

                lower = (1.0 - wx) * z00 + wx * z10
                upper = (1.0 - wx) * z01 + wx * z11
                grid[j, i] = (1.0 - wy) * lower + wy * upper
                filled[j, i] = true
            end
        end
    end

    missing_count = count(.!filled)
    missing_count == 0 ||
        error("Uniform DG sampling left $missing_count grid points uncovered")
    all(isfinite, grid) ||
        error("Uniform DG sampling produced non-finite values")

    return x_grid, y_grid, grid
end

# Maximum mirrored deviation, used by the t=0 renderer test.
function rti_max_mirrored_deviation(grid)
    ny, nx = size(grid)
    iseven(nx) || throw(ArgumentError("Mirror pairing requires an even Nx"))
    maximum_deviation = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:div(nx, 2)
        maximum_deviation = max(maximum_deviation,
                                abs(grid[j, i] - grid[j, nx + 1 - i]))
    end
    return maximum_deviation
end

# Backward-compatible name; the corrected test uses exact mirror pairs.
rti_max_x_deviation(grid) = rti_max_mirrored_deviation(grid)

# R_q = ||q - Mq||_2 / ||q||_2 on the complete cell-centered grid.
function rti_mirror_residual(grid)
    ny, nx = size(grid)
    iseven(nx) || throw(ArgumentError("Mirror pairing requires an even Nx"))

    numerator = 0.0
    denominator = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:div(nx, 2)
        q_left = grid[j, i]
        q_right = grid[j, nx + 1 - i]
        numerator += 2.0 * (q_left - q_right)^2
        denominator += q_left^2 + q_right^2
    end

    if denominator > 0.0
        return sqrt(numerator / denominator)
    end
    # An identically zero conservative variable, notably rho*u at t=0,
    # has zero residual rather than an undefined 0/0 value.
    return numerator == 0.0 ? 0.0 : NaN
end

# Four-connected component sizes. Periodic x-connectivity prevents a pocket
# crossing x=0/0.25 from being counted as two distinct physical structures.
function rti_component_sizes(mask; periodic_x=true)
    ny, nx = size(mask)
    visited = falses(ny, nx)
    sizes = Int[]
    stack = Int[]

    for start in eachindex(mask)
        if !mask[start] || visited[start]
            continue
        end

        visited[start] = true
        empty!(stack)
        push!(stack, start)
        component_size = 0

        while !isempty(stack)
            index = pop!(stack)
            component_size += 1
            j = mod(index - 1, ny) + 1
            i = div(index - 1, ny) + 1

            for (di, dj) in ((-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1))
                ii = i + di
                jj = j + dj

                if periodic_x
                    ii = mod(ii - 1, nx) + 1
                elseif ii < 1 || ii > nx
                    continue
                end
                if jj < 1 || jj > ny
                    continue
                end

                neighbor = jj + (ii - 1) * ny
                if mask[neighbor] && !visited[neighbor]
                    visited[neighbor] = true
                    push!(stack, neighbor)
                end
            end
        end
        push!(sizes, component_size)
    end

    sort!(sizes, rev=true)
    return sizes
end

function rti_structural_metrics(rho, x_grid, y_grid)
    ny, nx = size(rho)
    length(x_grid) == nx && length(y_grid) == ny ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Structural metric axes do not match rho"))

    mixing_mask = falses(ny, nx)
    heavy_mask = falses(ny, nx)
    light_mask = falses(ny, nx)
    upper_mixing_mask = falses(ny, nx)

    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        value = rho[j, i]
        in_mixing_zone = 1.1 < value < 1.9
        mixing_mask[j, i] = in_mixing_zone
        heavy_mask[j, i] = value > 1.9 && y_grid[j] > 0.5
        light_mask[j, i] = value < 1.1 && y_grid[j] < 0.5
        upper_mixing_mask[j, i] = in_mixing_zone && y_grid[j] > 0.5
    end

    mixing_zone_count = count(mixing_mask)
    dx = 0.25 / nx
    dy = 1.0 / ny

    # Central differences in periodic x and in interior y; one-sided
    # differences at the two wall-adjacent cell centers.
    sharpness_sum = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:nx
        if mixing_mask[j, i]
            i_left = i == 1 ? nx : i - 1
            i_right = i == nx ? 1 : i + 1
            drho_dx = (rho[j, i_right] - rho[j, i_left]) / (2.0 * dx)

            drho_dy = if j == 1
                (rho[2, i] - rho[1, i]) / dy
            elseif j == ny
                (rho[ny, i] - rho[ny - 1, i]) / dy
            else
                (rho[j + 1, i] - rho[j - 1, i]) / (2.0 * dy)
            end
            sharpness_sum += hypot(drho_dx, drho_dy)
        end
    end
    sharpness = mixing_zone_count > 0 ?
                sharpness_sum / mixing_zone_count : NaN

    heavy_sizes = rti_component_sizes(heavy_mask; periodic_x=true)
    light_sizes = rti_component_sizes(light_mask; periodic_x=true)
    upper_mixing_sizes =
        rti_component_sizes(upper_mixing_mask; periodic_x=true)

    return (
        mixing_zone_count=mixing_zone_count,
        mixing_zone_area=mixing_zone_count * dx * dy,
        sharpness=sharpness,
        heavy_pocket_count=length(heavy_sizes),
        light_pocket_count=length(light_sizes),
        structural_count=length(heavy_sizes) + length(light_sizes),
        heavy_pocket_sizes=heavy_sizes,
        light_pocket_sizes=light_sizes,
        # Deterministic density-based proxy for additional resolved
        # substructures in the upper mushroom/vortex region.
        upper_mixing_component_count=length(upper_mixing_sizes),
        upper_mixing_component_sizes=upper_mixing_sizes
    )
end

function rti_store_comparison_sample!(run, target_time,
                                      x_grid, y_grid, rho_grid, p_grid)
    state = run.diagnostics
    metrics = rti_structural_metrics(rho_grid, x_grid, y_grid)
    sample = (x=x_grid, y=y_grid, rho=rho_grid, p=p_grid, metrics=metrics)
    state.fixed_grid_samples[target_time] = sample

    filename = joinpath(state.npz_dir,
                        Printf.@sprintf("fixed_grid_time_%08.5f.npz",
                                        target_time))
    npzwrite(filename, Dict(
        "t" => target_time,
        "x" => x_grid,
        "y" => y_grid,
        "rho" => rho_grid,
        "p" => p_grid,
        "mixing_zone_count" => metrics.mixing_zone_count,
        "mixing_zone_area" => metrics.mixing_zone_area,
        "sharpness" => metrics.sharpness,
        "structural_count" => metrics.structural_count,
        "upper_mixing_component_count" =>
            metrics.upper_mixing_component_count
    ))
    return sample
end

# Compute and retain only residuals and the two comparison samples; large
# transient conservative grids at the other times are not retained.
function rti_compute_stage_diagnostic_data!(run;
                                            times=rti_symmetry_times,
                                            store_comparison=true)
    state = run.diagnostics

    for target_time in times
        filename = rti_snapshot_file(state, target_time)
        data = npzread(filename)
        sampling_options = (nx=rti_comparison_nx,
                            ny=rti_comparison_ny,
                            degree=run.polydeg,
                            cell_centered=true)

        rho_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho"; sampling_options...)
        rho_u_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_u";
                                               sampling_options...)
        rho_v_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_v";
                                               sampling_options...)
        rho_e_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_e";
                                               sampling_options...)

        x_grid, y_grid, rho_grid = rho_sample
        nx = length(x_grid)
        for i in 1:nx
            isapprox(x_grid[i] + x_grid[nx + 1 - i], 0.25;
                     rtol=0.0, atol=8.0 * eps(Float64)) ||
                error("Cell-centered mirror grid pairing is not exact")
        end

        residuals = (
            rho=rti_mirror_residual(rho_grid),
            rho_u=rti_mirror_residual(rho_u_sample[3]),
            rho_v=rti_mirror_residual(rho_v_sample[3]),
            rho_e=rti_mirror_residual(rho_e_sample[3])
        )
        state.symmetry_residuals[target_time] = residuals

        if store_comparison &&
           any(t -> isapprox(target_time, t;
                             rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-10),
               rti_comparison_times)
            p_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "p";
                                               sampling_options...)
            rti_store_comparison_sample!(run, target_time,
                                         x_grid, y_grid, rho_grid,
                                         p_sample[3])
        end
    end

    return state
end
```

```julia
function rti_print_timestep_audit(run)
    state = run.diagnostics
    audit = rti_timestep_audit(state)
    steps = state.accepted_steps

    println("\nTIMESTEP AUDIT: ", run.label)
    Printf.@printf("  raw accepted steps=%d; ordinary steps=%d; scheduled AMR events=%d (interval=%d)\n",
                   audit.accepted_step_count, audit.ordinary_step_count,
                   length(audit.amr_events), rti_amr_interval)
    Printf.@printf("  ordinary dt: min=%.16e, median=%.16e, max=%.16e\n",
                   audit.ordinary_min, audit.ordinary_median,
                   audit.ordinary_max)
    Printf.@printf("  final step separately: t=%.16e, dt=%.16e, dt<1e-8=%s\n",
                   audit.final_t, audit.final_dt,
                   string(audit.final_is_tiny))
    Printf.@printf("  initialization separately: t=%.16e, dt=%.16e\n",
                   audit.initialization_t, audit.initialization_dt)
    Printf.@printf("  steps with dt<1e-8: count=%d, coinciding with scheduled AMR events=%d\n",
                   audit.tiny_count, audit.tiny_amr_count)

    for step in audit.tiny_steps
        Printf.@printf("    tiny step: t=%.16e, tprev=%.16e, dt=%.16e, iter=%d, AMR_event=%s\n",
                       step.t, step.tprev, step.dt, step.iter,
                       string(step.amr_event))
    end

    if audit.tiny_count == 0
        println("  assessment: no accepted step below 1e-8")
    elseif audit.tiny_count == 1 && audit.final_is_tiny
        println("  assessment: tiny dt is confined to the final step; consistent with tspan clipping")
    else
        println("  assessment: recurring/non-final tiny steps require AMR/StepsizeCallback inspection")
    end

    # Preserve the unaveraged history used for every value above.
    if !isempty(steps)
        npzwrite(joinpath(state.npz_dir, "accepted_step_history.npz"), Dict(
            "t" => [step.t for step in steps],
            "tprev" => [step.tprev for step in steps],
            "dt" => [step.dt for step in steps],
            "iter" => [step.iter for step in steps],
            "amr_event" => [step.amr_event for step in steps],
            "initialization_t" => audit.initialization_t,
            "initialization_dt" => audit.initialization_dt
        ))
    end
    return audit
end

rti_maximum_residual(r) = max(r.rho, r.rho_u, r.rho_v, r.rho_e)

function rti_print_symmetry_monitor(run; times=rti_symmetry_times)
    state = run.diagnostics
    println("\nSYMMETRY MONITOR: ", run.label)
    println("  cell-centered grid=$(rti_comparison_nx)x$(rti_comparison_ny); no duplicated periodic endpoint")
    println("  mirror pairing: i <-> Nx+1-i; residual=||q-Mq||_2/||q||_2")
    println("  t | R_rho | R_rho_u | R_rho_v | R_rho_E")

    for target_time in times
        residuals = state.symmetry_residuals[target_time]
        all(isfinite,
            (residuals.rho, residuals.rho_u,
             residuals.rho_v, residuals.rho_e)) ||
            error("Non-finite symmetry residual for $(run.label) at t=$target_time")
        Printf.@printf("  %.2f | %.16e | %.16e | %.16e | %.16e\n",
                       target_time, residuals.rho, residuals.rho_u,
                       residuals.rho_v, residuals.rho_e)
    end

    if any(t -> t == 0.0, times)
        initial_maximum =
            rti_maximum_residual(state.symmetry_residuals[0.0])
        Printf.@printf("  t=0 diagnostic validation: max_residual=%.16e, tolerance=1.0e-12\n",
                       initial_maximum)
        initial_maximum <= 1.0e-12 ||
            error("The corrected t=0 symmetry diagnostic is not at machine precision")
    end
    return nothing
end

function rti_render_stage!(run)
    state = run.diagnostics

    # Use the same rasterization path for the renderer test and the panels.
    initial_snapshot = npzread(rti_snapshot_file(state, 0.0))
    initial_rho = rti_sample_uniform_grid(initial_snapshot, "rho";
                                          degree=run.polydeg)
    initial_p = rti_sample_uniform_grid(initial_snapshot, "p";
                                        degree=run.polydeg)
    rho_x_deviation = rti_max_mirrored_deviation(initial_rho[3])
    p_x_deviation = rti_max_mirrored_deviation(initial_p[3])
    Printf.@printf("Renderer t=0 x-independence (%s): max_density_deviation=%.16e, max_pressure_deviation=%.16e\n",
                   run.label, rho_x_deviation, p_x_deviation)

    sampled_snapshots = Any[]
    for target_time in visualization_times
        data = npzread(rti_snapshot_file(state, target_time))
        rho_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho";
                                             degree=run.polydeg)
        p_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "p";
                                           degree=run.polydeg)
        push!(sampled_snapshots,
              (x=rho_sample[1], y=rho_sample[2],
               rho=rho_sample[3], p=p_sample[3]))
    end

    style = (
        seriestype=:heatmap,
        aspect_ratio=:equal,
        xlims=(0.0, 0.25),
        ylims=(0.0, 1.0),
        yflip=false,
        axis=nothing,
        framestyle=:none,
        legend=false,
        colorbar=false,
        margin=2Plots.mm
    )

    plots_array = Any[]
    for (sampled, target_time) in
        zip(sampled_snapshots, visualization_times)
        push!(plots_array,
              heatmap(sampled.x, sampled.y, sampled.rho,
                      title="ρ, t=$(target_time)"; style...))
    end
    for (sampled, target_time) in
        zip(sampled_snapshots, visualization_times)
        push!(plots_array,
              heatmap(sampled.x, sampled.y, sampled.p,
                      title="p, t=$(target_time)"; style...))
    end

    stage_plot = plot(plots_array..., layout=(2, 4),
                      size=(3200, 2400), margin=5Plots.mm)
    mkpath(dirname(run.figure_file))
    savefig(stage_plot, run.figure_file)
    println("Stored $(run.label) panel figure: $(run.figure_file)")
    return run.figure_file
end

function rti_print_final_diagnostics(run)
    diagnostics = run.diagnostics.final_diagnostics
    diagnostics === nothing &&
        error("Final diagnostics were not recorded for $(run.label)")

    println("\nFinal diagnostic summary: ", run.label)
    Printf.@printf("  t=%.5f, relative_mass_drift=%.6e, min_rho=%.6e, min_p=%.6e\n",
                   diagnostics.t, diagnostics.relative_mass_drift,
                   diagnostics.rho_min, diagnostics.p_min)
    Printf.@printf("  max_abs_v_y0=%.6e, max_abs_v_y1=%.6e, accepted_steps=%d\n",
                   diagnostics.max_abs_v_y0, diagnostics.max_abs_v_y1,
                   diagnostics.accepted_step_count)
    return nothing
end

function rti_postprocess_stage!(run)
    println("\nPost-processing ", run.label)
    rti_compute_stage_diagnostic_data!(run)
    rti_render_stage!(run)
    rti_print_symmetry_monitor(run)
    rti_print_timestep_audit(run)
    rti_print_final_diagnostics(run)
    return nothing
end

function rti_maybe_run_symmetry_control(runs)
    reference_run = nothing
    largest_residual = -Inf

    for run in runs
        residuals = run.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0]
        residual = rti_maximum_residual(residuals)
        if residual > largest_residual
            largest_residual = residual
            reference_run = run
        end
    end

    if largest_residual <= 1.0e-2
        Printf.@printf("\nSYMMETRY CONTINGENCY: not triggered; largest corrected t=3 residual=%.6e <= 1.0e-2\n",
                       largest_residual)
        return nothing
    end

    Printf.@printf("\nSYMMETRY CONTINGENCY: triggered by %s with max t=3 residual=%.6e > 1.0e-2\n",
                   reference_run.label, largest_residual)
    control = rti_run_symmetric_fixed_mesh_control(reference_run)
    rti_compute_stage_diagnostic_data!(control;
                                       times=[0.0, 3.0],
                                       store_comparison=false)
    rti_print_symmetry_monitor(control; times=[0.0, 3.0])

    reference = reference_run.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0]
    fixed = control.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0]
    println("\nSYMMETRY CONTROL COMPARISON at t=3.0: AMR reference vs symmetric fixed mesh")
    for (name, amr_residual, fixed_residual) in (
        ("rho", reference.rho, fixed.rho),
        ("rho_u", reference.rho_u, fixed.rho_u),
        ("rho_v", reference.rho_v, fixed.rho_v),
        ("rho_E", reference.rho_e, fixed.rho_e)
    )
        Printf.@printf("  %s: AMR=%.16e, fixed_mesh=%.16e\n",
                       name, amr_residual, fixed_residual)
    end
    return control
end

function rti_relative_shift(baseline, candidate)
    if baseline == 0
        return candidate == 0 ? 0.0 : NaN
    end
    return (candidate - baseline) / abs(baseline)
end

rti_material_label(final_time, flag) =
    final_time ? string(flag) : "informational"

function rti_resolution_decision(stage_a, stage_b)
    println("\nRESOLUTION CHECK")
    println("  identical cell-centered grid: $(rti_comparison_nx)x$(rti_comparison_ny)")
    println("  metric | polydeg=5 | polydeg=6 | relative shift | material?")

    m1 = false
    m2 = false
    m3_count = false
    m3_new = false

    for target_time in rti_comparison_times
        metrics_a = stage_a.diagnostics.fixed_grid_samples[target_time].metrics
        metrics_b = stage_b.diagnostics.fixed_grid_samples[target_time].metrics
        final_time = isapprox(target_time, 3.0;
                              rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-12)

        area_shift = rti_relative_shift(metrics_a.mixing_zone_count,
                                        metrics_b.mixing_zone_count)
        sharpness_shift = rti_relative_shift(metrics_a.sharpness,
                                             metrics_b.sharpness)
        structure_shift = rti_relative_shift(metrics_a.structural_count,
                                             metrics_b.structural_count)
        substructure_shift =
            rti_relative_shift(metrics_a.upper_mixing_component_count,
                               metrics_b.upper_mixing_component_count)

        if final_time
            m1 = isfinite(area_shift) && abs(area_shift) > 0.10
            m2 = isfinite(sharpness_shift) && abs(sharpness_shift) > 0.10
            m3_count = metrics_a.structural_count !=
                       metrics_b.structural_count
            # Deterministic identical-grid proxy for additional resolved
            # substructures in the upper mushroom/vortex region.
            m3_new = metrics_b.upper_mixing_component_count >
                     metrics_a.upper_mixing_component_count
        end

        println("  t=$(target_time)")
        Printf.@printf("  A_mix [# cells] | %d | %d | %+.6e | %s\n",
                       metrics_a.mixing_zone_count,
                       metrics_b.mixing_zone_count, area_shift,
                       rti_material_label(final_time, m1))
        Printf.@printf("  A_mix physical area | %.16e | %.16e | %+.6e | %s\n",
                       metrics_a.mixing_zone_area,
                       metrics_b.mixing_zone_area, area_shift,
                       rti_material_label(final_time, m1))
        Printf.@printf("  S mean |grad(rho)| | %.16e | %.16e | %+.6e | %s\n",
                       metrics_a.sharpness, metrics_b.sharpness,
                       sharpness_shift,
                       rti_material_label(final_time, m2))
        Printf.@printf("  structural count | %d | %d | %+.6e | %s\n",
                       metrics_a.structural_count,
                       metrics_b.structural_count, structure_shift,
                       rti_material_label(final_time, m3_count))
        Printf.@printf("  upper-vortex mixing components | %d | %d | %+.6e | %s\n",
                       metrics_a.upper_mixing_component_count,
                       metrics_b.upper_mixing_component_count,
                       substructure_shift,
                       rti_material_label(final_time, m3_new))
    end

    m3 = m3_count || m3_new
    material = m1 || m2 || m3
    println("  M1 (A_mix shift >10% at t=3): ", m1)
    println("  M2 (S shift >10% at t=3): ", m2)
    println("  M3 (structural count change or new upper-vortex components): ",
            m3)

    if material
        println("VERDICT: MATERIAL — promoting polydeg=6, CFL=0.30 to production")
    else
        println("VERDICT: NOT MATERIAL — retaining polydeg=5, CFL=0.35 (Run 3 configuration)")
    end

    return (material=material, m1=m1, m2=m2, m3=m3)
end

println("Generating Rayleigh–Taylor diagnostics and stage summary plots...")

# Each stage is sampled and rendered once; no production run is repeated.
for run in rti_resolution_runs
    rti_postprocess_stage!(run)
end

# This is the only possible additional run, and it is diagnostic rather than
# a resolution-check repetition or a production candidate.
rti_symmetry_control =
    rti_maybe_run_symmetry_control(rti_resolution_runs)

rti_decision = rti_resolution_decision(rti_stage_a, rti_stage_b)
rti_selected_run = rti_decision.material ? rti_stage_b : rti_stage_a

isfile(rti_selected_run.figure_file) ||
    error("Selected stage figure is missing: $(rti_selected_run.figure_file)")
summary_path = joinpath(@__DIR__, "summary_all.png")
cp(rti_selected_run.figure_file, summary_path; force=true)

println("\nSelected production run: ", rti_selected_run.label)
println("Selected production configuration: polydeg=$(rti_selected_run.polydeg), CFL=$(rti_selected_run.cfl)")
println("Saved selected stage figure as summary_all.png")

selected_diagnostics = rti_selected_run.diagnostics.final_diagnostics
selected_symmetry =
    rti_selected_run.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0]
println("Final selected-run diagnostic summary:")
Printf.@printf("  t=%.5f, relative_mass_drift=%.6e, min_rho=%.6e, min_p=%.6e\n",
               selected_diagnostics.t,
               selected_diagnostics.relative_mass_drift,
               selected_diagnostics.rho_min,
               selected_diagnostics.p_min)
Printf.@printf("  max_abs_v_y0=%.6e, max_abs_v_y1=%.6e, max_t3_symmetry_residual=%.6e\n",
               selected_diagnostics.max_abs_v_y0,
               selected_diagnostics.max_abs_v_y1,
               rti_maximum_residual(selected_symmetry))
println("Done.")
```

Inspector summary:
 Numerical implementation of the 2D inviscid compressible Euler Rayleigh-Taylor instability using DGSEM. Stage A uses polydeg=5, CFL=0.35, HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing with flux_ranocha DG and flux_lax_friedrichs FV, density-based Löhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel 4/5/6 at thresholds 0.1/0.3 and interval=20, RDPK3SpFSAL49 with adaptive=false, and tspan=(0.0,3.0) on [0,0.25]x[0,1] with periodic x and slip y walls. Stage B repeats the same configuration with polydeg=6 and CFL=0.30 as the single non-repeating resolution check. The code regenerates the polydeg=5 baseline, computes both stages on the identical 512x2048 cell-centered comparison grid at t=2.25 and t=3.0, evaluates the M1/M2/M3 materiality gate, prints the explicit RESOLUTION CHECK verdict, and selects summary_all.png from the already-rendered Stage A or Stage B 2x4 rho/p panel figure. It also implements the raw accepted-step timestep audit, per-variable mirror-indexed symmetry residuals at t=0,0.75,1.5,2.25,3.0, mass drift/positivity/wall diagnostics, and the conditional fixed-mesh symmetry control run. The initial conditions, pressure law, gravity source term, boundary topology, and required diagnostics match the strategy.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

**Output:**
Create an appropiate Figure and saved it as summary_all.png

## Section 1: Status Decision

**Status:** `REJECTED_FOR_REFINEMENT`

The selected `polydeg=6, CFL=0.30` solution is high fidelity, but the final symmetry report is not internally consistent and prevents a perfect score. This is primarily a **verification-diagnostic rejection**, not a request to change the numerical architecture.

The saturation condition is also not met: Run 2 was the first run scoring at least 90, and only Run 3 and the present run have followed it—fewer than five subsequent refinement trials.

## Section 2: Quantitative Scorecard

- **Score Integrity:** 50/50
- **Score Accuracy:** 19/20
- **Score Details:** 15/15
- **Score Optimality:** 15/15
- **TOTAL SCORE:** **99/100**

**Reference-error check:** The supplied log tail contains no `Minimum achieved error:` line. **Quantitative error is not applicable as no ground truth is available.** In particular, the reported symmetry residual is an internal diagnostic, not an error against an analytical solution.

## Section 3: Forensic Analysis & Feedback

### Observation

- The density evolution is physically coherent:
  - At \(t=0.75\), the seeded single-mode displacement is visible.
  - At \(t=1.5\), the central heavy-fluid spike and adjacent light-fluid bubbles have developed.
  - At \(t=2.25\), the spike forms a mushroom-like head with rolled-up side structures.
  - At \(t=3.0\), secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz structures and interaction with the upper wall are visible. The interfaces remain thin and highly detailed, without obvious Gibbs ringing or catastrophic grid-scale oscillations.
- Pressure retains the expected early-time hydrostatic stratification. Late-time pressure variations and upper-wall compression are consistent with the increasingly nonlinear dynamics.
- The selected run completed successfully and saved the figure. Positivity and conservation are strong:
  - Relative mass drift is approximately \(7.27\times10^{-12}\).
  - Minimum density and pressure remain positive: \(\rho_{\min}\approx0.1921\), \(p_{\min}\approx1.2083\).
  - Wall-normal velocity maxima are small, approximately \(5.63\times10^{-5}\) at \(y=0\) and \(3.73\times10^{-4}\) at \(y=1\).
- Promotion of Stage B is supported by the resolution check. At \(t=3\), mixing-zone area changes by only about \(-1.75\%\), while mean interfacial sharpness increases by approximately \(20.7\%\). Thus, the higher-degree run resolves sharper small-scale structure without materially changing the overall mixing-zone area.
- The final `max_t3_symmetry_residual=2.000000e+00` is suspicious rather than evidence of complete physical symmetry failure. The plotted solution remains approximately mirror-symmetric, and the reported even-field residuals are small: the AMR residuals for \(\rho v\) and \(\rho E\) are approximately \(5.18\times10^{-5}\) and \(1.40\times10^{-6}\), respectively.

### Diagnosis

**Likely reflection-parity bug in the symmetry diagnostic.**

Under reflection about \(x=0.125\), the conserved state transforms as

\[
(\rho,\rho u,\rho v,\rho E)
\longmapsto
(\rho,-\rho u,\rho v,\rho E).
\]

Thus, \(\rho u\) is **odd**, while \(\rho\), \(\rho v\), and \(\rho E\) are **even**. If the diagnostic computes \(\|q-Mq\|/\|q\|\) for every variable, then for an odd momentum field satisfying \(Mq=-q\),

\[
\frac{\|q-Mq\|}{\|q\|}
=
\frac{\|2q\|}{\|q\|}
=2.
\]

The value exactly equal to 2 is therefore the expected signature of applying an even-field formula to \(\rho u\). It should not be interpreted as a physical loss of symmetry. The fixed-mesh control run was consequently probably triggered by a faulty diagnostic rather than by meaningful AMR-induced asymmetry.

The raw connected-component increase is not, by itself, proof of convergence because pixel-scale fragments can inflate that count. Nevertheless, the independent sharpness improvement and the visually coherent additional roll-ups justify retaining Stage B.

No exact-method override is warranted: nonlinear two-dimensional compressible Euler with gravitational forcing, wall interaction, and Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-up does not admit a feasible closed-form, Fourier-series, or global method-of-characteristics solution.

### Targeted Improvements

- **For Integrity:** None for the solver execution. Both stages completed, the selected figure was saved, and the reported state remains finite and positive.
- **For Accuracy:** Correct the symmetry residual using parity-aware reflections:
  \[
  R_{\rho u}=\frac{\|\rho u+M(\rho u)\|}{\|\rho u\|},
  \qquad
  R_q=\frac{\|q-Mq\|}{\|q\|}
  \quad\text{for }q=\rho,\rho v,\rho E.
  \]
  Handle the initially zero \(\rho u\) norm with an absolute-residual check or a guarded denominator. Recompute these diagnostics from the archived Stage B full state; another PDE solve is unnecessary unless the corrected residuals reveal genuine asymmetry. Do not trigger further fixed-mesh control runs from the uncorrected maximum residual.
- **For Details:** Retain `polydeg=6, CFL=0.30`, HLLC, and the existing AMR limits. Further degree or mesh increases are not justified by the present evidence. For future resolution gates, apply a minimum physical-area threshold to connected components so that grid-scale fragments are not counted as newly resolved vortices.
- **For Optimality:** None. DGSEM with contact-resolving HLLC flux, pressure-based shock capturing, and density-based AMR remains well matched to this problem.

**Recommendation:** Retain Stage B as the current best production dataset. The next iteration should close the parity-aware symmetry verification rather than repeat or further refine the already successful simulation.
