--- 1. Calling Strategist Team ---

=== 🧠 Starting Strategy Team (Proposer-Critic Loop) ===

--- 🔄 Strategy Iteration 1/5 ---
--- Strategy Report Received ---
**1. Problem Statement**

Single-mode inviscid compressible Rayleigh–Taylor instability: a heavy fluid ($\rho=2$) rests *beneath* a light fluid ($\rho=1$) while a body force $g=+1$ acts in $+y$, pushing heavy fluid into light fluid — the mirror image of the classical configuration, hence unstable. A single-mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ perturbation seeds the interface at $y=0.5$.

**This iteration is a verification-closure, NOT a refinement.** The Stage B production configuration (polydeg=6, CFL=0.30, HLLC, AMR 4/5/6) is **retained verbatim**. The single required change is the correction of the symmetry diagnostic to **parity-aware reflection residuals**, recomputed from the archived Stage B state. The reported residual of exactly $2.0$ on $\rho u$ is the mathematical signature of applying an even-field formula to an odd field ($\|q-(-q)\|/\|q\|=2$), i.e., evidence of *perfect* symmetry, not symmetry loss.

---

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D Compressible Euler with gravitational source:
$$\partial_t \mathbf{U} + \partial_x \mathbf{F}(\mathbf{U}) + \partial_y \mathbf{G}(\mathbf{U}) = \mathbf{S}(\mathbf{U}), \qquad \mathbf{U} = \begin{pmatrix}\rho \\ \rho u \\ \rho v \\ \rho E\end{pmatrix}$$
$$\mathbf{F} = \begin{pmatrix}\rho u \\ \rho u^2 + p \\ \rho u v \\ (\rho E + p)\,u\end{pmatrix}, \qquad \mathbf{G} = \begin{pmatrix}\rho v \\ \rho u v \\ \rho v^2 + p \\ (\rho E + p)\,v\end{pmatrix}, \qquad \mathbf{S} = \begin{pmatrix}0 \\ 0 \\ \rho g \\ \rho g v\end{pmatrix}$$
with $p = (\gamma-1)\left(\rho E - \tfrac{1}{2}\rho(u^2+v^2)\right)$, $\gamma = 1.4$, $g = 1.0$ (positive $y$).

* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0,\,0.25] \times [0,\,1.0]$, $t \in [0,\,3.0]$.

* **Boundary Conditions:** $x$: Periodic. $y=0$ and $y=1$: Reflective / free-slip walls (no-penetration $v=0$; mirror reflection of tangential velocity).

* **Initial Conditions** (interface at $y=0.5$):
$$\rho(x,y,0) = 2.0 + (1.0 - 2.0)\cdot\tfrac{1}{2}\left[1 + \tanh\left(50(y-0.5)\right)\right]$$
$$p(y,0) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} \qquad (\text{continuous at } y=0.5:\ p=2.0)$$
$$u(x,y,0) = 0$$
$$v(x,y,0) = -0.025\,\sqrt{\frac{\gamma\, p(y)}{\rho(y)}}\;\cos(8\pi x)\,\left[\sin(\pi y)\right]^6$$
$$\rho E = \frac{p}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\rho\left(u^2 + v^2\right)$$

* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; gravity vector $\mathbf{g} = [0,\,1.0]^T$; $\rho_{heavy}=2.0$, $\rho_{light}=1.0$; perturbation amplitude $0.025$; mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ (wavelength $=0.25$, single mode across periodic width); $\mu = 0$, $k = 0$.

* **Expected Physics:** Heavy-fluid **spike rises at $x=0.125$** (where $v>0$ initially); light-fluid **bubbles descend at $x=0 \equiv 0.25$**. $t\approx0.75$: visible modal displacement; $t\approx1.5$: spike + bubbles developed; $t\approx2.25$: mushroom head with rolled-up side structures; $t\approx3.0$: secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-ups and spike interaction with the upper wall $y=1$. The exact solution is mirror-symmetric about $x=0.125$: under $x \to 0.25 - x$, $(\rho, \rho u, \rho v, \rho E) \mapsto (\rho, -\rho u, \rho v, \rho E)$.

---

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Can we obtain an exact solution?** **No.** This is nonlinear multi-dimensional compressible Euler with a gravitational source, wall boundaries, and vortex roll-up. The Method of Characteristics is restricted to 1D/simple-wave structures — in 2D the characteristic families interact non-trivially, gravity destroys Riemann-invariant reductions, and KH roll-up generates multi-valued caustics immediately. Fourier methods require periodicity in *all* directions (violated by the slip walls in $y$) and global smoothness (violated by contacts, shocks, and roll-up). No Green's function / separation-of-variables reduction exists for the nonlinear system. The Advisor explicitly confirms: no exact-method override is warranted.
* **Selection:** **DGSEM 2D plus time**, with the Stage B configuration retained verbatim. This iteration modifies only the *verification diagnostic*, not the discretization.

---

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** Use template defaults, **except** the Advisor-mandated retentions in §3.1. Do not change the time stepper or any parameter not listed there.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing:**
  * **Variable Selection Analysis:** The structural features of RT (interface, spikes, bubbles, mushroom roll-ups) are **contact discontinuities**, across which pressure is *continuous* — a pressure-based AMR indicator would be blind to the dominant feature. Velocity $(u,v)$ becomes globally chaotic in the late-time mixing zone (KH turbulence) and would scatter refinement elements everywhere. **Density $\rho$** remains sharp and strictly localized at the features of interest for the entire run → **AMR indicator: density** (Löhner). This is strictly superior for this PDE.
  * **Shock Capturing indicator: pressure** (retained per Advisor) — compressive features (wall impact, late-time acoustic compression) are pressure phenomena, while contact-rich interface elements remain pure-DG with HLLC contact resolution.

* **3.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides):**
  1. **RETAIN Stage B verbatim:** polydeg=6, CFL=0.30, HLLC surface flux, AMR `ControllerThreeLevel(base=4, med=5, max=6)` with thresholds 0.1/0.3, interval=20. **No further degree, mesh, or CFL increases.**
  2. **PARITY-AWARE SYMMETRY DIAGNOSTIC (the core fix).** With $M$ = reflection about $x=0.125$ ($x \to 0.25-x$):
     $$R_{\rho u} = \frac{\|\rho u + M(\rho u)\|}{\|\rho u\|} \quad (\text{odd field}), \qquad R_q = \frac{\|q - M(q)\|}{\|q\|} \quad \text{for } q = \rho,\ \rho v,\ \rho E \ (\text{even fields}).$$
     Guard the initially-zero $\rho u$ norm: use $\max(\|\rho u\|, \varepsilon)$ or fall back to the absolute residual $\|\rho u + M(\rho u)\|$ when $\|\rho u\| < \varepsilon$. The value $R=2.0$ from the old formula on $\rho u$ is the signature of *perfect* symmetry, not failure.
  3. **RECOMPUTE, DON'T RE-SOLVE:** Recompute the corrected residuals at $t = 0,\,0.75,\,1.5,\,2.25,\,3.0$ plus the standard suite (mass drift, min $\rho/p$ positivity, wall $|v|$) **from the archived Stage B full state**. A new PDE solve is unnecessary unless corrected residuals reveal genuine asymmetry. If the archive is unavailable in the execution environment, re-run the *identical* Stage B configuration (a reproduction, not a refinement).
  4. **NO fixed-mesh control runs** may be triggered from the uncorrected maximum residual. Any control-run trigger must key on the corrected parity-aware residuals only (genuine asymmetry $\equiv$ corrected $R \gtrsim 10^{-2}$, far above the $\sim10^{-5}$ discretization noise floor).
  5. **Connected-component census filter:** any mixing-zone/vortex component counting must apply a **minimum physical-area threshold** so grid-scale fragments are not counted as newly resolved vortices.

---

**4. Visualization Strategy**

* **Field Selection (Analysis):** **Density $\rho$** — the primary driver; the interface, spikes, bubbles, and mixing zone are density features. **Pressure $p$** — secondary; hydrostatic stratification and late-time wall compression. Do **not** plot $u, v, E$ or derived quantities.
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ (top row) and $p$ (bottom row) at $t = 0.75,\ 1.5,\ 2.25,\ 3.0$.
* **Layout:** Grid, 2 rows (fields) × 4 columns (time).
* **Saving Data:** Save the **full solution state** (all conserved variables) at frequent intervals (`dt_save=0.1`) — required so the parity-aware diagnostics can be recomputed post hoc from the archive.
* **Constraints:** Save as `summary_all.png` (strictly), rendered from the **Stage B (polydeg=6)** solution. **Julia: `colorbar=false` (explicitly disable colorbars).** Automatic per-panel color scaling; no fixed colormaps/clims; no mesh lines.

---

**5. Cross-Validation & Data Integration (Conditional)**

* **Trigger check:** No external data paths (sensors / true data / reference solution) are provided in [USER_QUESTION] → **Not applicable.**
* The parity-aware symmetry residual is an *internal verification* diagnostic. Print the corrected values with explicit labels; expected result: corrected $R_{\rho u} \approx 0$ (not 2.0) and even-field residuals consistent with the previously reported $\approx 5.2\times10^{-5}$ ($\rho v$) and $\approx 1.4\times10^{-6}$ ($\rho E$), confirming symmetry and closing the verification loop.

---

**REQUIRED COMPONENTS**

**Main Method:**
* Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time

**Enhancements:**
* Shock Capturing (pressure-based indicator)
* Adaptive Mesh Refinement (density-based indicator)
* Parity-Aware Symmetry Diagnostic (Advisor-mandated)
* Connected-Component Minimum-Area Filter (Advisor-mandated)

--- Critic report---
APPROVED

**Validation:** (1) **Method scope/formatting:** The Main Method is exactly “Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time”; all diagnostics, AMR, and shock capturing are correctly listed separately as enhancements. (2) **Physics consistency:** The equations, positive-\(y\) gravity source, \(\gamma=1.4\), domain, periodic/\(y\)-wall boundaries, and inviscid setting are consistent with the problem. (3) **Exact-solution priority:** Section 2 explicitly establishes why an exact/MoC or global Fourier solution is infeasible for this nonlinear, forced, wall-bounded 2D Euler problem, so DGSEM is permissible. (4) **Redundancy and micromanagement:** Retaining Stage B is an Advisor-mandated verification closure with a corrected diagnostic, not an unchanged repetition seeking a different solver result; the conditional fallback re-solve is justified, and retained numerical parameters are explicitly Advisor-mandated. (5) **Data validation:** No external ground-truth files are supplied, so Section 5 correctly marks external cross-validation inapplicable and identifies symmetry as an internal diagnostic. --- 3. Calling Implementation Team ---
--- Starting Sequential, Stateful Refinement ---
Planner Study Phase: 1. **Context:** The template is a Trixi DGSEM/MOL Rayleigh–Taylor solver with pressure-based shock capturing, density-based AMR, full-state NPZ archiving, and post-processing; this iteration retains the Stage B numerics while making verification archive-first, parity-aware, and area-filtered.

2. **Mapping:**
   - Cell 1 (Solver and Discretization Initialization): [Keep] - Existing imports and thread initialization are sufficient; no environment or solver-package changes are needed.

```julia
# Recover the conservative-state size from archived element-block data. This
# is used only to size the conditional fixed-mesh diagnostic control.
function rti_archived_stage_b_state_dofs(data, degree)
    rho = data["rho"]
    n_elements = 0

    if ndims(rho) == 3
        n_elements = size(rho, 3)
    elseif ndims(rho) == 2
        nrows, ncols = size(rho)
        candidates = (degree, degree + 1, 2 * degree,
                      2 * degree + 1, 2 * (degree + 1))
        for n in candidates
            if n >= 2 && nrows == n * n
                n_elements = ncols
                break
            elseif n >= 2 && nrows == n && ncols % n == 0
                n_elements = div(ncols, n)
                break
            end
        end
    end

    n_elements > 0 ||
        error("Could not recover Stage B's archived element count")
    return 4 * n_elements * (degree + 1)^2
end

# Archive-first verification closure. The five snapshots required for the
# corrected symmetry residuals also include every requested plotting time.
function rti_load_archived_stage_b()
    label = "stage_b_polydeg6"
    degree = 6
    output_root = joinpath(@__DIR__, label)
    stage_npz_dir = joinpath(output_root, "data_npz")
    stage_trixi_dir = joinpath(output_root, "trixi_output")
    isdir(stage_npz_dir) || return nothing

    diagnostics = rti_new_stage_diagnostics(label, degree, stage_npz_dir)

    for target_time in rti_symmetry_times
        filename = joinpath(
            stage_npz_dir,
            Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
        isfile(filename) || return nothing

        data = npzread(filename)
        archived_degree = data["polydeg"]
        archived_degree = archived_degree isa Number ?
                          Int(archived_degree) : Int(only(archived_degree))
        archived_degree == degree || return nothing

        diagnostics.snapshot_files[target_time] = filename
    end

    final_data = npzread(diagnostics.snapshot_files[3.0])
    state_dofs = rti_archived_stage_b_state_dofs(final_data, degree)

    # final_diagnostics remains uninitialized here; the post-processing cell
    # recomputes mass drift, positivity, and wall diagnostics from the archive.
    return (
        label=label,
        polydeg=degree,
        cfl=0.30,
        semi=nothing,
        sol=nothing,
        diagnostics=diagnostics,
        npz_dir=stage_npz_dir,
        trixi_dir=stage_trixi_dir,
        figure_file=joinpath(output_root, "summary.png"),
        fixed_refinement_level=nothing,
        state_dofs=state_dofs
    )
end

rti_archived_stage_b = rti_load_archived_stage_b()
if rti_archived_stage_b === nothing
    println("Stage B archive unavailable; reproducing the identical production configuration: polydeg=6, CFL=0.30")
    rti_stage_b =
        rti_run_independent_stage("stage_b_polydeg6", 6, 0.30)
else
    println("Using archived Stage B full state: polydeg=6, CFL=0.30; no PDE solve is required")
    rti_stage_b = rti_archived_stage_b
end

# This iteration has no resolution refinement or Stage A production run.

# Conditional diagnostic only. Cell 12 may call this exclusively after the
# corrected parity-aware residuals establish genuine asymmetry.
function rti_run_symmetric_fixed_mesh_control(reference_run)
    degree = reference_run.polydeg
    target_state_dofs = reference_run.state_dofs
    target_state_dofs > 0 ||
        error("The reference run has no valid conservative-state size")

    # Select the uniform level whose total conservative-state size is closest
    # to the reference run's final size. Main-run AMR limits remain unchanged.
    levels = collect(4:6)
    candidate_state_dofs = [
        4 * (2^level)^2 * (degree + 1)^2 for level in levels
    ]
    level = levels[argmin(abs.(candidate_state_dofs .- target_state_dofs))]

    label = "symmetric_fixed_control_polydeg$(degree)_level$(level)"
    println("SYMMETRY CONTINGENCY: running one fixed-mesh control without AMR")
    control = rti_run_independent_stage(label, degree, reference_run.cfl;
                                        fixed_refinement_level=level)
    Printf.@printf("Fixed-mesh control: level=%d, target_state_dofs=%d, control_state_dofs=%d\n",
                   level, target_state_dofs, control.state_dofs)
    return control
end
```

- New Code (lines 163-183):
```julia
# Parity-aware reflection residual on the complete cell-centered grid:
# even: ||q - Mq||_2 / ||q||_2
# odd:  ||q + Mq||_2 / ||q||_2
function rti_mirror_residual(grid; parity=:even, norm_epsilon=1.0e-14)
    ny, nx = size(grid)
    iseven(nx) || throw(ArgumentError("Mirror pairing requires an even Nx"))
    parity in (:even, :odd) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("parity must be :even or :odd"))

    numerator = 0.0
    denominator = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:div(nx, 2)
        q_left = grid[j, i]
        q_right = grid[j, nx + 1 - i]
        deviation = parity === :odd ?
                    q_left + q_right : q_left - q_right
        numerator += 2.0 * deviation^2
        denominator += q_left^2 + q_right^2
    end

    absolute_residual = sqrt(numerator)
    field_norm = sqrt(denominator)

    # In particular, rho*u is identically zero at t=0. In that case report
    # ||rho*u + M(rho*u)||_2 rather than forming an undefined 0/0 ratio.
    if field_norm < norm_epsilon
        return absolute_residual
    end
    return absolute_residual / field_norm
end
```

```julia
    cell_area = dx * dy
    # A census component must cover at least 16 raster cells. Applying the
    # threshold to component area, rather than raw pixel count alone, prevents
    # grid-scale fragments from being interpreted as resolved vortices.
    minimum_component_area = 16.0 * cell_area

    heavy_sizes = filter(
        n -> n * cell_area >= minimum_component_area,
        rti_component_sizes(heavy_mask; periodic_x=true))
    light_sizes = filter(
        n -> n * cell_area >= minimum_component_area,
        rti_component_sizes(light_mask; periodic_x=true))
    upper_mixing_sizes = filter(
        n -> n * cell_area >= minimum_component_area,
        rti_component_sizes(upper_mixing_mask; periodic_x=true))

    return (
        mixing_zone_count=mixing_zone_count,
        mixing_zone_area=mixing_zone_count * dx * dy,
        sharpness=sharpness,
        minimum_component_area=minimum_component_area,
        heavy_pocket_count=length(heavy_sizes),
        light_pocket_count=length(light_sizes),
        structural_count=length(heavy_sizes) + length(light_sizes),
        heavy_pocket_sizes=heavy_sizes,
        light_pocket_sizes=light_sizes,
        # Deterministic, area-filtered density-based proxy for additional
        # resolved substructures in the upper mushroom/vortex region.
        upper_mixing_component_count=length(upper_mixing_sizes),
        upper_mixing_component_sizes=upper_mixing_sizes
    )
```

```julia
function rti_print_symmetry_monitor(run; times=rti_symmetry_times)
    state = run.diagnostics
    println("\nPARITY-AWARE SYMMETRY MONITOR: ", run.label)
    println("  cell-centered grid=$(rti_comparison_nx)x$(rti_comparison_ny); no duplicated periodic endpoint")
    println("  mirror pairing: i <-> Nx+1-i, reflection about x=0.125")
    println("  even fields: R_q=||q-Mq||_2/||q||_2 for rho, rho_v, rho_E")
    println("  odd field: R_rho_u=||rho_u+M(rho_u)||_2/||rho_u||_2")
    println("  zero-norm guard: absolute residual when ||q||_2 < 1.0e-14")
    println("  t | R_rho (even) | R_rho_u (odd) | R_rho_v (even) | R_rho_E (even)")

    for target_time in times
        residuals = state.symmetry_residuals[target_time]
        all(isfinite,
            (residuals.rho, residuals.rho_u,
             residuals.rho_v, residuals.rho_e)) ||
            error("Non-finite symmetry residual for $(run.label) at t=$target_time")
        Printf.@printf("  %.2f | %.16e | %.16e | %.16e | %.16e\n",
                       target_time, residuals.rho, residuals.rho_u,
                       residuals.rho_v, residuals.rho_e)
    end

    if any(t -> t == 0.0, times)
        initial_maximum =
            rti_maximum_residual(state.symmetry_residuals[0.0])
        Printf.@printf("  t=0 corrected diagnostic validation: max_residual=%.16e, tolerance=1.0e-12\n",
                       initial_maximum)
        initial_maximum <= 1.0e-12 ||
            error("The corrected t=0 symmetry diagnostic is not at machine precision")
    end
    return nothing
end
```

- New Code (lines 147-170):
```julia
# Interpolatory quadrature weights on the actual archived element nodes.
# This supports both LGL output and finer visualization-node output while
# integrating the archived element polynomial without a rasterized mass proxy.
function rti_archive_quadrature_weights(nodes)
    n = length(nodes)
    n >= 2 || throw(ArgumentError("At least two element nodes are required"))
    lower, upper = extrema(nodes)
    upper > lower || throw(ArgumentError("Degenerate archived element nodes"))

    reference_nodes = [
        2.0 * (node - lower) / (upper - lower) - 1.0 for node in nodes
    ]
    weights = zeros(Float64, n)

    for k in 1:n
        # Coefficients of the kth Lagrange polynomial in ascending powers.
        coefficients = [1.0]
        denominator = 1.0

        for m in 1:n
            m == k && continue
            updated = zeros(Float64, length(coefficients) + 1)
            for d in 1:length(coefficients)
                updated[d] -= reference_nodes[m] * coefficients[d]
                updated[d + 1] += coefficients[d]
            end
            coefficients = updated
            denominator *= reference_nodes[k] - reference_nodes[m]
        end

        iszero(denominator) &&
            throw(ArgumentError("Repeated archived interpolation node"))

        # Integrate each monomial over the reference interval [-1,1].
        for d in 1:length(coefficients)
            if isodd(d)
                weights[k] += coefficients[d] * (2.0 / d) / denominator
            end
        end
    end
    return weights
end

function rti_archived_mass(data, degree)
    x_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["x"], degree)
    y_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["y"], degree)
    rho_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["rho"], degree)
    size(x_blocks) == size(y_blocks) == size(rho_blocks) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Archived mass arrays have inconsistent shapes"))

    total_mass = 0.0
    for element in axes(x_blocks, 3)
        x_first = view(x_blocks, :, 1, element)
        x_second = view(x_blocks, 1, :, element)
        first_spread = maximum(x_first) - minimum(x_first)
        second_spread = maximum(x_second) - minimum(x_second)

        if first_spread >= second_spread
            x_nodes = collect(x_first)
            y_nodes = collect(view(y_blocks, 1, :, element))
            rho_block = Array(view(rho_blocks, :, :, element))
        else
            x_nodes = collect(x_second)
            y_nodes = collect(view(y_blocks, :, 1, element))
            rho_block = permutedims(
                Array(view(rho_blocks, :, :, element)))
        end

        x_order = sortperm(x_nodes)
        y_order = sortperm(y_nodes)
        x_nodes = x_nodes[x_order]
        y_nodes = y_nodes[y_order]
        rho_block = rho_block[x_order, y_order]

        x_weights = rti_archive_quadrature_weights(x_nodes)
        y_weights = rti_archive_quadrature_weights(y_nodes)
        hx = x_nodes[end] - x_nodes[1]
        hy = y_nodes[end] - y_nodes[1]

        element_mass = 0.0
        for j in eachindex(y_weights), i in eachindex(x_weights)
            element_mass += rho_block[i, j] *
                            x_weights[i] * y_weights[j]
        end

        # Reference-element Jacobian for an axis-aligned quadtree element.
        total_mass += 0.25 * hx * hy * element_mass
    end
    return total_mass
end

function rti_recompute_archived_diagnostics!(run)
    state = run.diagnostics
    final_time = tspan[2]
    initial_data = npzread(rti_snapshot_file(state, 0.0))
    final_data = npzread(rti_snapshot_file(state, final_time))

    initial_mass = rti_archived_mass(initial_data, run.polydeg)
    final_mass = rti_archived_mass(final_data, run.polydeg)
    state.initial_mass = initial_mass
    mass_drift = final_mass - initial_mass

    rho_min, rho_max = extrema(final_data["rho"])
    p_min, p_max = extrema(final_data["p"])

    # The archive contains conservative variables, so recover v=rho_v/rho.
    y = vec(final_data["y"])
    v = vec(final_data["rho_v"] ./ final_data["rho"])
    wall_bottom = rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v, 0.0)
    wall_top = rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v, 1.0)

    dt_last = isempty(state.accepted_steps) ?
              NaN : state.accepted_steps[end].dt

    state.final_diagnostics = (
        t=final_time,
        mass=final_mass,
        mass_drift=mass_drift,
        relative_mass_drift=abs(mass_drift / initial_mass),
        rho_min=rho_min,
        rho_max=rho_max,
        p_min=p_min,
        p_max=p_max,
        dt_last=dt_last,
        initialization_dt=state.initialization_dt,
        accepted_step_count=length(state.accepted_steps),
        max_abs_v_y0=wall_bottom,
        max_abs_v_y1=wall_top
    )
    return nothing
end

function rti_print_final_diagnostics(run)
    diagnostics = run.diagnostics.final_diagnostics
    diagnostics === nothing &&
        error("Final diagnostics were not recorded for $(run.label)")

    println("\nFinal diagnostic summary recomputed from archived full state: ",
            run.label)
    Printf.@printf("  t=%.5f, mass_drift=%.6e, relative_mass_drift=%.6e, min_rho=%.6e, min_p=%.6e\n",
                   diagnostics.t, diagnostics.mass_drift,
                   diagnostics.relative_mass_drift,
                   diagnostics.rho_min, diagnostics.p_min)
    Printf.@printf("  max_abs_v_y0=%.6e, max_abs_v_y1=%.6e, accepted_steps=%d\n",
                   diagnostics.max_abs_v_y0, diagnostics.max_abs_v_y1,
                   diagnostics.accepted_step_count)
    return nothing
end

function rti_postprocess_stage!(run)
    println("\nPost-processing ", run.label)
    rti_compute_stage_diagnostic_data!(run)
    rti_recompute_archived_diagnostics!(run)
    rti_render_stage!(run)
    rti_print_symmetry_monitor(run)

    if isempty(run.diagnostics.accepted_steps)
        println("\nTIMESTEP AUDIT: ", run.label,
                " skipped; archive-only snapshots contain no accepted-step history")
    else
        rti_print_timestep_audit(run)
    end

    rti_print_final_diagnostics(run)
    return nothing
end
```

--- 4. Calling Debugging Team ---
Inspector summary:
 Implementation is a 2D DGSEM plus explicit time integration for the compressible Euler equations with gamma=1.4 and a +y gravitational source. Domain is [0,0.25]x[0,1], t in [0,3]. Stage B is selected: polydeg=6, CFL=0.30, HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing, density-based Löhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel base=4 med=5 max=6, thresholds 0.1/0.3, interval=20. Initial conditions match the strategy exactly: tanh density interface at y=0.5, piecewise hydrostatic pressure, zero u, v=-0.025 sqrt(gamma p/rho) cos(8*pi*x) sin(pi*y)^6, and prim2cons energy. y-walls are slip/reflective; x is periodic. The code first attempts to load archived Stage B npz full states at t=0,0.75,1.5,2.25,3.0; if absent it reproduces the identical Stage B configuration. It computes parity-aware reflection residuals on a cell-centered 512x2048 grid: even for rho, rho v, rho E; odd for rho u, with zero-norm absolute fallback. It prints residuals and standard diagnostics (mass drift, min rho/p, wall |v|), applies a connected-component minimum-area filter, and triggers a fixed-mesh control only if corrected t=3 max residual exceeds 1e-2. It renders rho (top row) and p (bottom row) at the four requested times with colorbar=false and copies the Stage B figure to summary_all.png.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

## Section 3: Forensic Analysis & Feedback

### Observation

* The simulation completed through \(t=3\), produced a readable `summary_all.png`, and retained positive density and pressure:
  * \(\min\rho=0.1920849\), \(\min p=1.208287\).
  * Relative mass drift is only \(7.25\times10^{-12}\).
  * Wall-normal velocity maxima are small: \(5.63\times10^{-5}\) at \(y=0\) and \(3.73\times10^{-4}\) at \(y=1\).
* The density evolution is physically consistent with the prescribed unstable configuration:
  * At \(t=0.75\), the single-mode interface displacement is visible.
  * At \(t=1.5\), the central heavy-fluid spike and adjacent light-fluid bubbles are developed.
  * At \(t=2.25\), the mushroom head and rolled-up side structures appear.
  * At \(t=3\), secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz structures and interaction with the upper wall are visible.
* The structures remain visually symmetric about \(x=0.125\). The corrected parity-aware residual at \(t=3\), \(1.126302\times10^{-4}\), supports this assessment and correctly does not trigger the fixed-mesh control run. The former residual of \(2.0\) on \(\rho u\) was indeed a diagnostic-parity artifact, not evidence of symmetry loss.
* Density interfaces and vortex roll-ups are well resolved, without conspicuous Gibbs ringing or excessive large-scale smearing. Pressure remains predominantly stratified at early times and develops plausible late-time compressional structure.
* The only remaining integrity concern is the timestep audit: **11 non-final steps with \(dt<10^{-8}\)**, several with \(dt\sim10^{-16}\), occur at scheduled event times. Their magnitude indicates floating-point event-time roundoff rather than a physical CFL collapse, but the log itself correctly requests inspection of the AMR/stepsize callback interaction.

### Diagnosis

**Solver event-scheduling roundoff**, not a PDE stability or accuracy failure. Duplicate or nearly coincident save/AMR event times appear to leave a remaining interval of a few machine epsilons, which is then taken as a separate accepted step. No further mesh, polynomial-degree, or flux refinement is justified by the present plots or diagnostics.

### Targeted Improvements

* **For Integrity:** Fix the event/timestep bookkeeping while retaining the Stage B discretization:
  * De-duplicate scheduled save and AMR event times and treat event-time differences within a few ULPs as coincident.
  * When the remaining distance to an event is roundoff-sized, treat the event as reached rather than enqueueing another step.
  * Inspect `AMRCallback` and `StepsizeCallback` interaction so neither forces a non-final step below the audit floor.
  * Add a regression check requiring **zero non-final accepted steps with \(dt<10^{-8}\)**; continue to allow a genuinely necessary short final step to reach \(t=3\).
  * Re-run the identical configuration once after this control-flow fix and confirm that conservation, positivity, wall behavior, symmetry, and the rendered fields remain unchanged.
* **For Accuracy:** **None.** Retain `polydeg=6`, CFL \(=0.30\), HLLC, and the existing initial/boundary conditions and gravity source.
* **For Details:** **None.** Retain density-based AMR and pressure-based shock capturing; the present interfaces and roll-ups do not justify more aggressive refinement or dissipation.
* **For Optimality:** **None.** No exact analytical method is available for this nonlinear, multidimensional, gravity-driven Euler problem with wall interaction and vortex roll-up. DGSEM with HLLC, contact-resolving AMR, and localized shock capturing remains an appropriate high-fidelity fallback.

**Recommendation:** Address only the roundoff-sized event steps. Do not increase polynomial degree, AMR levels, or mesh resolution, and do not reduce CFL merely to suppress this bookkeeping artifact.

=== 🧠 Starting Strategy Team (Proposer-Critic Loop) ===

--- 🔄 Strategy Iteration 1/5 ---
--- Strategy Report Received ---
**1. Problem Statement**

High-fidelity simulation of the single-mode Rayleigh–Taylor Instability (RTI) governed by the 2D inviscid compressible Euler equations with a gravitational source. A heavy fluid ($\rho=2$) lies beneath a light fluid ($\rho=1$) with effective gravity $g=+\hat{y}$, rendering the interface at $y=0.5$ unstable; a single-mode velocity perturbation seeds growth through spike/bubble formation, mushroom roll-up, and secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz structures up to $t=3.0$.

**Status:** This is a *control-flow refinement iteration* of an already-validated solver. Five prior DGSEM trials reached 99/100 with correct physics, conservation (mass drift $\sim 7\times10^{-12}$), positivity, wall compliance, and symmetry. The sole remaining defect is a bookkeeping artifact: **11 non-final accepted steps with $dt < 10^{-8}$ (several $\sim 10^{-16}$) at scheduled event times** — floating-point event-time roundoff, not CFL collapse. The discretization is frozen; the task is to repair the event/step scheduling, gate it with a regression check, and re-run the identical configuration.

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D Compressible Euler with gravity, conservative variables $\mathbf{U} = (\rho, \rho u, \rho v, E)^T$:
$$\partial_t \mathbf{U} + \partial_x \begin{pmatrix} \rho u \\ \rho u^2 + p \\ \rho u v \\ (E+p)u \end{pmatrix} + \partial_y \begin{pmatrix} \rho v \\ \rho u v \\ \rho v^2 + p \\ (E+p)v \end{pmatrix} = \begin{pmatrix} 0 \\ 0 \\ \rho g \\ \rho g v \end{pmatrix}, \qquad E = \frac{p}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\rho(u^2 + v^2)$$

* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$; $t \in [0, 3.0]$.

* **Boundary Conditions:** $x = 0,\ 0.25$: **Periodic**. $y = 0,\ 1$: **Reflective / free-slip walls** (no-penetration $v = 0$).

* **Initial Conditions** (exact formulas, interface at $y=0.5$):
$$\rho(x,y) = 2.0 - \frac{1}{2}\left[1 + \tanh\left(50\,(y - 0.5)\right)\right]$$
$$p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} \quad (\text{continuous at } y=0.5:\ p=2.0;\ dp/dy = \rho g \text{ on both sides})$$
$$u(x,y) = 0$$
$$v(x,y) = -0.025\; c(x,y)\, \cos(8\pi x)\, \sin^6(\pi y), \qquad c(x,y) = \sqrt{\frac{\gamma\, p(y)}{\rho(x,y)}}$$
$$E(x,y) = \frac{p(y)}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\,\rho(x,y)\, v(x,y)^2$$

* **Constants & Parameters:** $\gamma = 1.4$; $g = 1.0$ in $+y$ (source $(0, 0, \rho g, \rho g v)$); $\rho_{heavy}=2.0$, $\rho_{light}=1.0$; interface $y_c = 0.5$; perturbation amplitude $0.025$; mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ (wavelength $\lambda = 0.25$); $\mu = 0$, $k = 0$ (inviscid, non-conducting).

* **Expected Physics:** $t\approx0.75$: single-mode interface displacement; $t\approx1.5$: central heavy-fluid spike rising ($+y$) with adjacent light-fluid bubbles; $t\approx2.25$: mushroom head and rolled-up vortices; $t=3.0$: secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz roll-ups and interaction with the upper wall. Mirror symmetry about $x=0.125$ throughout. **Regression baselines (must be reproduced):** relative mass drift $\sim 7\times10^{-12}$; $\min\rho \approx 0.192$, $\min p \approx 1.208$; wall $\max|v| \approx 5.6\times10^{-5}$ ($y=0$), $3.7\times10^{-4}$ ($y=1$); parity-corrected symmetry residual at $t=3$ $\approx 1.1\times10^{-4}$.

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Can we obtain an exact solution?** **No.** The RTI is a nonlinear, multi-dimensional, gravitationally forced hyperbolic system with an unstable contact discontinuity. The Method of Characteristics is tractable only for 1D/simple-wave problems; here the single-mode perturbation produces crossing characteristics, multi-valued regions, vortex roll-up, and secondary KH instability with no closed-form solution. Linear theory yields only the early-time growth rate, not field evolution to $t=3$. The Advisor explicitly confirms no exact analytical method exists for this configuration.
* **Selection:** **DGSEM in 2D plus time** — validated at 99/100 over five trials — remains the Main Method. This iteration changes *only* the event-scheduling control flow; the discretization is frozen bit-for-bit.

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** **Do NOT use template defaults — the Advisor has pinned the configuration** (identical Stage B re-run): DGSEM (Lobatto–Legendre), **polydeg = 6**; surface flux **HLLC**; shock capturing via **pressure-based IndicatorHennemannGassner** (VolumeIntegralShockCapturingHG, volume_flux_dg = flux_ranocha, volume_flux_fv = flux_lax_friedrichs); **density-based IndicatorLöhner AMR** with ControllerThreeLevel (base_level=4, med_level=5 @ 0.1, max_level=6 @ 0.3), interval = 20, adapt_initial_condition = true; time integration RDPK3SpFSAL49, adaptive = false, **CFL = 0.30**, tspan = (0.0, 3.0). Do NOT increase polydeg/AMR levels/mesh resolution; do NOT reduce CFL; do NOT change fluxes, indicators, ICs, BCs, or the gravity source.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing — Variable Selection Analysis:**
    * *Feature-defining variable:* **Density**. The heavy/light material interface (contact, $\rho: 2 \to 1$) and its rolled-up filaments are the structures of interest; $\rho$ remains sharp and localized at these features for the entire run.
    * *Noisy variables:* Velocity $(u,v)$ becomes globally chaotic inside the late-time KH mixing layer — refining on it over-refines turbulent bulk flow and wastes elements. Pressure is smooth/hydrostatic early and carries broad acoustic content late — refining on it chases sound waves.
    * *Selection:* **AMR on density (Löhner)** — superior because it tracks exactly the interface and roll-up structures without over-refining chaotic or acoustic regions; **shock capturing on pressure** — detects compressive fronts. This pairing is retained from the validated runs.

* **3.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides):**
    1. **Event-time de-duplication:** Build the sorted union of all time-scheduled events (solution saves, any time-scheduled AMR/checkpoint events, and the final time $t=3.0$); merge event times separated by $\lesssim$ a few ULPs (e.g., $|\Delta t| \le 10\,\varepsilon_{\text{mach}}\cdot\max(1,|t|)$) into a single event.
    2. **Roundoff-safe step acceptance:** When the remaining distance to the next event is roundoff-sized (e.g., $\le 10^{-12}$ — physically negligible vs. CFL steps $\sim 10^{-3}$), treat the event as **reached**: snap $t$ to the event time and fire callbacks. Never enqueue a new accepted step whose sole purpose is covering a few-ULP gap.
    3. **Callback interaction audit:** Inspect the `AMRCallback`/`StepsizeCallback` interaction so neither forces a non-final step below the audit floor; AMR re-scheduling must not create near-duplicate event times with save times.
    4. **Regression gate:** Post-run audit of raw accepted steps must show **zero non-final accepted steps with $dt < 10^{-8}$**; a genuinely necessary short *final* step landing exactly on $t = 3.0$ remains allowed. Print explicit PASS/FAIL.
    5. **Identical re-run + baseline comparison:** After the control-flow fix, re-run the identical Stage B configuration once and confirm (to roundoff/rendering tolerance) unchanged: mass drift $\sim 7\times10^{-12}$; $\min\rho \approx 0.192$, $\min p \approx 1.208$; wall $\max|v|$ as listed above; parity-corrected symmetry residual $\approx 1.1\times10^{-4}$ at $t=3$ (below the $10^{-2}$ fixed-mesh control trigger); and unchanged rendered $\rho/p$ fields.

**4. Visualization Strategy:**

* **Field Selection (Analysis):** **$\rho$ (density)** and **$p$ (pressure)**. Density is the primary driver and tracer of RT dynamics — it delineates the interface, spike/bubble morphology, and KH filaments. Pressure captures the hydrostatic stratification and late-time compressional structure. Velocity is chaotic in the mixing layer and is omitted for readability.
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ (top row) and $p$ (bottom row) at $t = 0.75,\ 1.5,\ 2.25,\ 3.0$.
* **Layout:** Grid, 2 rows (fields) $\times$ 4 columns (times).
* **Saving Data:** Save the **full solution state** (all conservative variables) at frequent intervals, `dt_save = 0.1`, plus $t=0$ and the four visualization times (merged and de-duplicated per §3.1) for Ground Truth.
* **Constraints:** Save as `summary_all.png` (strict). **DISABLE COLORBARS** (`colorbar = false`) — Julia. Automatic per-panel color scaling (no fixed ranges). Plot only primary variables $\rho, p$; no derived quantities.

**5. Cross-Validation & Data Integration (Conditional)**

* No external reference/sensor data paths are provided → no external cross-validation. Cell 8: Data I/O & Surrogate Interoperability

- Action: Exact `unique` cannot merge event times separated by a few ULPs. Replace it with a canonical sorted event schedule containing the NPZ/`SaveSolutionCallback` times, the integrator’s `saveat=0.01` output times, and exactly \(t=3.0\). AMR remains iteration-scheduled and therefore contributes no additional time stamps. Also provide the roundoff-gap predicate for the scheduling changes in Cells 9–10.
- New Code (lines 15-16):
```julia
rti_event_merge_ulps = 10.0
rti_event_reached_atol = 1.0e-12

@inline function rti_event_ulp_tolerance(t)
    return rti_event_merge_ulps * eps(Float64) * max(1.0, abs(t))
end

@inline function rti_same_event(t, other)
    return abs(t - other) <= max(rti_event_ulp_tolerance(t),
                                 rti_event_ulp_tolerance(other))
end

function rti_dedup_event_times(times; final_time=3.0)
    final = Float64(final_time)
    isfinite(final) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("The final event time must be finite"))

    candidates = Float64[final]
    for value in times
        t = Float64(value)
        isfinite(t) ||
            throw(ArgumentError("Scheduled event times must be finite"))
        push!(candidates, t)
    end
    sort!(candidates)

    merged = Float64[]
    for t in candidates
        if isempty(merged) || !rti_same_event(t, merged[end])
            push!(merged, t)
        elseif t == final || merged[end] == final
            # Preserve an exact canonical final time for the regression gate.
            merged[end] = final
        end
        # Otherwise retain the existing canonical representative.
    end
    return merged
end

# A remaining distance at this scale is event-time roundoff, not a CFL step.
@inline function rti_event_reached(t, event_time)
    tolerance = max(rti_event_reached_atol,
                    rti_event_ulp_tolerance(t),
                    rti_event_ulp_tolerance(event_time))
    return abs(event_time - t) <= tolerance
end

requested_save_times = vcat(collect(0.0:dt_save:3.0),
                            visualization_times)
requested_solution_save_times = collect(0.0:0.01:3.0)

# Shared union of all time-scheduled output events and the final time.
rti_scheduled_event_times = rti_dedup_event_times(
    vcat(requested_save_times, requested_solution_save_times);
    final_time=3.0)

# Canonicalize each output schedule against that same union so separate
# callbacks cannot reintroduce ULP-separated copies of an event.
function rti_canonical_event_subset(times, canonical_times)
    subset = Float64[]
    for value in times
        t = Float64(value)
        index = argmin(abs.(canonical_times .- t))
        event = canonical_times[index]
        rti_same_event(event, t) ||
            error("Missing canonical representative for event t=$t")
        push!(subset, event)
    end
    sort!(subset)
    unique!(subset) # Exact duplicates after canonicalization only.
    return subset
end

save_times = rti_canonical_event_subset(requested_save_times,
                                        rti_scheduled_event_times)
rti_solution_save_times =
    rti_canonical_event_subset(requested_solution_save_times,
                               rti_scheduled_event_times)
```

- Action: Preserve the raw accepted-step recorder unchanged, but extend its audit to distinguish non-final violations from a genuinely tiny final step. Expose a Boolean regression result without hiding or deleting any recorded steps.
- New Code (lines 228-256):
```julia
# Return statistics without discarding the raw history. Only the last raw
# accepted step may be excused, and only when it lands exactly on final_time.
function rti_timestep_audit(state; final_time=3.0)
    steps = state.accepted_steps
    ordinary_dts = Float64[]
    nonfinal_tiny_steps = NamedTuple[]

    for i in 1:max(length(steps) - 1, 0)
        step = steps[i]
        push!(ordinary_dts, step.dt)
        if step.dt < 1.0e-8
            push!(nonfinal_tiny_steps, step)
        end
    end

    tiny_steps = filter(step -> step.dt < 1.0e-8, steps)
    tiny_amr_count = count(step -> step.amr_event, tiny_steps)
    final_is_tiny = !isempty(steps) && steps[end].dt < 1.0e-8
    final_at_tspan = !isempty(steps) && steps[end].t == final_time

    regression_pass = !isempty(steps) &&
                      isempty(nonfinal_tiny_steps) &&
                      (!final_is_tiny || final_at_tspan)

    return (
        accepted_step_count=length(steps),
        ordinary_step_count=length(ordinary_dts),
        ordinary_min=isempty(ordinary_dts) ? NaN : minimum(ordinary_dts),
        ordinary_median=isempty(ordinary_dts) ? NaN : median(ordinary_dts),
        ordinary_max=isempty(ordinary_dts) ? NaN : maximum(ordinary_dts),
        final_t=isempty(steps) ? NaN : steps[end].t,
        final_dt=isempty(steps) ? NaN : steps[end].dt,
        final_is_tiny=final_is_tiny,
        final_at_tspan=final_at_tspan,
        initialization_t=state.initialization_t,
        initialization_dt=state.initialization_dt,
        tiny_count=length(tiny_steps),
        tiny_amr_count=tiny_amr_count,
        tiny_steps=tiny_steps,
        nonfinal_tiny_count=length(nonfinal_tiny_steps),
        nonfinal_tiny_amr_count=
            count(step -> step.amr_event, nonfinal_tiny_steps),
        nonfinal_tiny_steps=nonfinal_tiny_steps,
        regression_pass=regression_pass,
        amr_events=state.amr_events
    )
end
```

- New Code (lines 26-54):
```julia
function rti_build_stage_callbacks(semi_stage, amr_stage, io_stage;
                                   cfl, output_directory)
    summary = SummaryCallback()
    analysis = AnalysisCallback(semi_stage, interval=analysis_interval)
    alive = AliveCallback(analysis_interval=analysis_interval)

    # Retain Trixi's output initialization, file counter, and save affect,
    # but disable its independent step-interval save trigger. In particular,
    # do not use SaveSolutionCallback(dt=...), whose PeriodicCallback would
    # accumulate a separate, ULP-shifted sequence of time events.
    save_template = SaveSolutionCallback(
        interval=typemax(Int),
        save_initial_solution=false,
        save_final_solution=false,
        output_directory=output_directory,
        solution_variables=cons2cons)

    # Use exactly the same canonical event representatives as the NPZ output.
    # save_times includes t=0, dt_save output, visualization times, and t=3.
    save = PresetTimeCallback(save_times, save_template.affect!;
                              save_positions=(false, false))

    stepsize = StepsizeCallback(cfl=cfl)

    # save_template performs initialization/finalization only; save performs
    # all scheduled output. AMR remains iteration-scheduled, and the CFL
    # callback remains last so it sees any mesh changed by AMR.
    callback_set = if amr_stage === nothing
        # Used only by the conditional symmetric fixed-mesh control run.
        CallbackSet(summary, analysis, alive, save_template, save,
                    io_stage, stepsize)
    else
        CallbackSet(summary, analysis, alive, save_template, save,
                    amr_stage, io_stage, stepsize)
    end

    return (callbacks=callback_set,
            summary_callback=summary,
            analysis_callback=analysis,
            alive_callback=alive,
            save_solution=save,
            stepsize_callback=stepsize)
end
```

--- Analizing: # ## Cell 10: Time Integration Loop ---
  - [Planner]: Instructions received:
Cell 10: Time Integration Loop

- Action: Replace the independent `saveat=0.01` schedule with its canonical equivalent and explicitly stop at every de-duplicated event. Prepend a discrete scheduling callback that snaps a step landing within the roundoff tolerance of an event to that exact event before the existing save, AMR, diagnostic, and CFL callbacks are processed. The callback only changes roundoff-sized time discrepancies; it does not alter the solution, CFL step size, or AMR schedule.
- New Code (lines 15-26):
```julia
function rti_roundoff_event_target(integrator)
    t = integrator.t
    tprev = integrator.tprev
    t > tprev || return nothing

    for event in rti_scheduled_event_times
        # An event already reached on the preceding step must not be fired
        # again or cause time to be snapped backward on a later step.
        event <= tprev && continue

        if event > t && !rti_event_reached(t, event)
            break
        end

        if event != t && rti_event_reached(t, event)
            return event
        end
    end
    return nothing
end

function rti_snap_roundoff_event!(integrator)
    target = rti_roundoff_event_target(integrator)
    target === nothing && return nothing

    # The state difference over at most 1e-12 time units is roundoff-level
    # event bookkeeping. Snapping t before the remaining callbacks makes all
    # scheduled callbacks observe the same canonical event and prevents a
    # separate accepted step whose only purpose is crossing that gap.
    integrator.t = target
    return nothing
end

function rti_add_roundoff_event_scheduling(stage_callbacks)
    event_callback = DiscreteCallback(
        (u, t, integrator) ->
            rti_roundoff_event_target(integrator) !== nothing,
        rti_snap_roundoff_event!;
        save_positions=(false, false))

    # Preserve the existing callback ordering, with event canonicalization
    # first. Thus AMR remains iteration-scheduled and StepsizeCallback remains
    # last after any AMR mesh modification.
    return CallbackSet(stage_callbacks.continuous_callbacks...,
                       event_callback,
                       stage_callbacks.discrete_callbacks...)
end

function rti_solve_stage(ode_stage, stage_callbacks)
    scheduled_callbacks =
        rti_add_roundoff_event_scheduling(stage_callbacks)

    solution = solve(ode_stage, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
                     dt=1.0,
                     adaptive=false,
                     callback=scheduled_callbacks,
                     saveat=rti_solution_save_times,
                     tstops=filter(t -> t > tspan[1],
                                   rti_scheduled_event_times),
                     maxiters=1e7)
    isempty(solution.t) && error("Time integration returned no saved times")
    isapprox(solution.t[end], tspan[2]; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-12) ||
        error("Time integration stopped at t = $(solution.t[end]), expected $(tspan[2])")
    return solution
end
```

- New Code (lines 416-425):
```julia
function rti_compare_stage_b_baseline(run, baseline)
    println("\nSTAGE B BASELINE COMPARISON")

    if baseline === nothing
        println("  Previous archive unavailable; rendered-field comparison skipped")
        println("  Specified references: relative_mass_drift≈7.25e-12, " *
                "min_rho≈0.1920849, min_p≈1.208287")
        println("  wall references: max_abs_v_y0≈5.63e-5, " *
                "max_abs_v_y1≈3.73e-4, t=3 symmetry≈1.126302e-4")
        return nothing
    end

    # Only t=3 is needed for the baseline symmetry residual. Mass, positivity,
    # and wall diagnostics are recomputed from the archived t=0/t=3 states.
    rti_compute_stage_diagnostic_data!(baseline;
                                       times=[3.0],
                                       store_comparison=false)
    rti_recompute_archived_diagnostics!(baseline)

    current = run.diagnostics.final_diagnostics
    reference = baseline.diagnostics.final_diagnostics
    current_symmetry = rti_maximum_residual(
        run.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0])
    reference_symmetry = rti_maximum_residual(
        baseline.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0])

    comparisons = (
        ("relative_mass_drift", current.relative_mass_drift,
         reference.relative_mass_drift, 1.0e-12, 5.0e-2),
        ("min_rho", current.rho_min, reference.rho_min,
         1.0e-8, 1.0e-8),
        ("min_p", current.p_min, reference.p_min,
         1.0e-8, 1.0e-8),
        ("max_abs_v_y0", current.max_abs_v_y0,
         reference.max_abs_v_y0, 1.0e-9, 1.0e-5),
        ("max_abs_v_y1", current.max_abs_v_y1,
         reference.max_abs_v_y1, 1.0e-9, 1.0e-5),
        ("t3_symmetry", current_symmetry, reference_symmetry,
         1.0e-8, 1.0e-5)
    )

    comparison_pass = current.rho_min > 0.0 && current.p_min > 0.0
    println("  positivity of fresh archived rho/p: ",
            comparison_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")

    for (name, value, target, atol, rtol) in comparisons
        metric_pass = isfinite(value) &&
                      isapprox(value, target; atol=atol, rtol=rtol)
        comparison_pass = comparison_pass && metric_pass
        Printf.@printf("  %s: fresh=%.16e, baseline=%.16e, |delta|=%.3e, %s\n",
                       name, value, target, abs(value - target),
                       metric_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
    end

    # Use the renderer's default sampling resolution and endpoint-inclusive
    # grid, rather than the higher-resolution symmetry diagnostic grid.
    for target_time in visualization_times
        fresh_data = npzread(rti_snapshot_file(run.diagnostics, target_time))
        baseline_data =
            npzread(rti_snapshot_file(baseline.diagnostics, target_time))

        for variable in ("rho", "p")
            fresh_grid = rti_sample_uniform_grid(
                fresh_data, variable; degree=run.polydeg)[3]
            baseline_grid = rti_sample_uniform_grid(
                baseline_data, variable; degree=baseline.polydeg)[3]

            if size(fresh_grid) != size(baseline_grid)
                comparison_pass = false
                println("  rendered $variable at t=$target_time: FAIL " *
                        "(grid dimensions differ)")
                continue
            end

            difference = maximum(abs.(fresh_grid .- baseline_grid))
            scale = max(1.0, maximum(abs.(baseline_grid)))
            tolerance = 1.0e-10 + 1.0e-8 * scale
            field_pass = isfinite(difference) && difference <= tolerance
            comparison_pass = comparison_pass && field_pass

            Printf.@printf("  rendered %s, t=%.2f: max_abs_delta=%.3e, tolerance=%.3e, %s\n",
                           variable, target_time, difference, tolerance,
                           field_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
        end
    end

    println("STAGE B BASELINE COMPARISON: ",
            comparison_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
    return comparison_pass
end

println("Generating Stage B parity-aware verification and summary plot...")

# Only the fresh Stage B production dataset is post-processed. No Stage A
# solve or resolution-refinement decision is performed in this iteration.
rti_postprocess_stage!(rti_stage_b)

# Enforce the regression gate on the fresh run's unaveraged accepted steps.
rti_stage_b_audit = rti_timestep_audit(rti_stage_b.diagnostics;
                                       final_time=tspan[2])
println("\nTIMESTEP REGRESSION GATE: ",
        rti_stage_b_audit.regression_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
rti_stage_b_audit.regression_pass ||
    error("Timestep regression gate failed; inspect accepted_step_history.npz")

rti_stage_b_baseline_status =
    rti_compare_stage_b_baseline(rti_stage_b, rti_stage_b_baseline)
```

Report: There are discrepancies with the base strategy:

The strategy requires saving full state at dt_save=0.1 plus t=0 and the four visualization times; it does not request 0.01-spaced events. Cell 8: Data I/O & Surrogate Interoperability
- Action: Remove the unrequested 0.01-spaced solution-save schedule, which introduces extra `saveat`/`tstops` events and changes the validated Stage B control flow.
- Action: Build the canonical event union only from the required `dt_save=0.1` output, \(t=0\), visualization times, and final time. Reuse that schedule for ODE solution saves so Cell 10 receives no additional events.

New Code (lines 62-92):
```julia
requested_save_times = vcat(collect(0.0:dt_save:3.0),
                            visualization_times)

# Shared union of all requested output events and the final time. AMR remains
# iteration-scheduled and therefore contributes no additional time events.
rti_scheduled_event_times = rti_dedup_event_times(requested_save_times;
                                                  final_time=3.0)

# Canonicalize each output schedule against that same union so separate
# callbacks cannot reintroduce ULP-separated copies of an event.
function rti_canonical_event_subset(times, canonical_times)
    subset = Float64[]
    for value in times
        t = Float64(value)
        index = argmin(abs.(canonical_times .- t))
        event = canonical_times[index]
        rti_same_event(event, t) ||
            error("Missing canonical representative for event t=$t")
        push!(subset, event)
    end
    sort!(subset)
    unique!(subset) # Exact duplicates after canonicalization only.
    return subset
end

save_times = rti_canonical_event_subset(requested_save_times,
                                        rti_scheduled_event_times)
# Use exactly the required output events for saveat as well. In particular,
# do not introduce a finer independent solution-save schedule.
rti_solution_save_times = save_times
```

Cell 12: Post-Processing & Temporal Diagnostics
- Action: When the archived Stage B run is unavailable, actively check the fresh diagnostics against the specified numerical references. Return a Boolean result with explicit per-metric and overall PASS/FAIL output instead of returning `nothing`. Fallback tolerances account for the precision of the supplied reference values.

New Code (lines 402-409):
```julia
    if baseline === nothing
        println("  Previous archive unavailable; checking fresh diagnostics against the specified numerical references")
        println("  Rendered-field comparison: SKIP (no archived Stage B fields)")

        current = run.diagnostics.final_diagnostics
        current === nothing &&
            error("Final diagnostics are unavailable for $(run.label)")
        current_symmetry = rti_maximum_residual(
            run.diagnostics.symmetry_residuals[3.0])

        # These references are rounded numerical baselines. Their tolerances
        # include the supplied reference precision as well as roundoff-level
        # reproduction error.
        comparisons = (
            ("relative_mass_drift", current.relative_mass_drift,
             7.25e-12, 1.0e-12, 5.0e-2),
            ("min_rho", current.rho_min,
             0.1920849, 1.0e-6, 1.0e-6),
            ("min_p", current.p_min,
             1.208287, 1.0e-6, 1.0e-6),
            ("max_abs_v_y0", current.max_abs_v_y0,
             5.63e-5, 1.0e-7, 1.0e-3),
            ("max_abs_v_y1", current.max_abs_v_y1,
             3.73e-4, 1.0e-6, 1.0e-3),
            ("t3_symmetry", current_symmetry,
             1.126302e-4, 1.0e-8, 1.0e-4)
        )

        comparison_pass = current.rho_min > 0.0 && current.p_min > 0.0
        println("  positivity of fresh archived rho/p: ",
                comparison_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")

        for (name, value, target, atol, rtol) in comparisons
            metric_pass = isfinite(value) &&
                          isapprox(value, target; atol=atol, rtol=rtol)
            comparison_pass = comparison_pass && metric_pass
            Printf.@printf("  %s: fresh=%.16e, reference=%.16e, |delta|=%.3e, %s\n",
                           name, value, target, abs(value - target),
                           metric_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
        end

        println("STAGE B BASELINE COMPARISON (numerical reference fallback): ",
                comparison_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
        return comparison_pass
    end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements a 2D compressible Euler Rayleigh-Taylor simulation with DGSEM (Lobatto-Legendre), HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing, density-based Löhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel levels 4/5/6 and interval 20, and RDPK3SpFSAL49 time integration with adaptive=false, CFL=0.30, tspan=(0.0,3.0). The executed Stage B run uses polydeg=6. Initial conditions, gravity source, periodic x boundaries, and slip-wall y boundaries match the strategy. The code includes event-time deduplication, roundoff-safe event snapping, a timestep audit gate, baseline comparison, and a 2x4 summary_all.png plot. However, critical data-saving and diagnostic-printing requirements are not correctly satisfied.
Report: There are discrepancies with the base strategy:
The implementation violates the full-state data-saving requirement: in rti_save_npz_file, PlotData2D variables are extracted with getindex.(pd_cons.data, i) and getindex.(pd_prim.data, i). In Trixi, pd.data is a vector of variable arrays, so this call returns a length-4 vector of the i-th scalar from each variable rather than the full element-block array. Consequently the saved NPZ keys rho, rho_u, rho_v, rho_e, and p do not contain the required full solution state and downstream diagnostics cannot read the intended shapes. Additionally, the strategy requires printing all diagnostics at every save event, including the parity-aware symmetry residual. The save callback only prints mass, mass drift, positivity, and wall max|v|; symmetry residuals are computed only post-run at t=0, 0.75, 1.5, 2.25, and 3.0, not at every dt_save=0.1 save event.

```julia
# Save-time symmetry diagnostics require the rasterization routines before
# the time-integration cell is executed.
function rti_linear_bracket(nodes, value)
    n = length(nodes)
    n > 0 || throw(ArgumentError("Cannot interpolate an empty node sequence"))
    if n == 1
        return 1, 1, 0.0
    end
    left = clamp(searchsortedlast(nodes, value), 1, n - 1)
    right = left + 1
    spacing = nodes[right] - nodes[left]
    weight = spacing > 0.0 ?
             clamp((value - nodes[left]) / spacing, 0.0, 1.0) : 0.0
    return left, right, weight
end

function rti_snapshot_polydeg(data, degree=nothing)
    degree !== nothing && return Int(degree)
    if haskey(data, "polydeg")
        value = data["polydeg"]
        return value isa Number ? Int(value) : Int(only(value))
    end
    return polydeg
end

# PlotData2D normally supplies three-dimensional element blocks. Also accept
# flattened serializations using the polynomial degree of the current stage.
function rti_element_blocks(array, degree::Integer)
    ndims(array) == 3 && return array
    ndims(array) == 2 ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Expected DG element-block plot data"))
    nrows, ncols = size(array)
    candidates = (degree, degree + 1, 2 * degree,
                  2 * degree + 1, 2 * (degree + 1))
    for n in candidates
        if n >= 2 && nrows == n * n
            return reshape(array, n, n, ncols)
        elseif n >= 2 && nrows == n && ncols % n == 0
            return reshape(array, n, n, div(ncols, n))
        end
    end
    throw(ArgumentError("Could not recover DG visualization element blocks"))
end
rti_element_blocks(array) = rti_element_blocks(array, polydeg)

function rti_grid_axes(nx, ny; cell_centered=false)
    if cell_centered
        dx = 0.25 / nx
        dy = 1.0 / ny
        # No duplicated periodic endpoint: x_i + x_(Nx+1-i) = 0.25.
        x_grid = [(i - 0.5) * dx for i in 1:nx]
        y_grid = [(j - 0.5) * dy for j in 1:ny]
    else
        x_grid = collect(range(0.0, 0.25, length=nx))
        y_grid = collect(range(0.0, 1.0, length=ny))
    end
    return x_grid, y_grid
end

function rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, variable;
                                 nx=256, ny=1024,
                                 degree=nothing, cell_centered=false)
    (nx > 1 && ny > 1) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Uniform grid dimensions must exceed one"))
    degree_local = rti_snapshot_polydeg(data, degree)
    x_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["x"], degree_local)
    y_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data["y"], degree_local)
    z_blocks = rti_element_blocks(data[variable], degree_local)
    size(x_blocks) == size(y_blocks) == size(z_blocks) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Coordinates and solution data must have matching shapes"))

    x_grid, y_grid = rti_grid_axes(nx, ny; cell_centered=cell_centered)
    grid = fill(NaN, ny, nx)
    filled = falses(ny, nx)

    for element in axes(x_blocks, 3)
        x_block = view(x_blocks, :, :, element)
        y_block = view(y_blocks, :, :, element)
        x_min, x_max = extrema(x_block)
        y_min, y_max = extrema(y_block)

        # Determine which block dimension represents x.
        x_first = view(x_blocks, :, 1, element)
        x_second = view(x_blocks, 1, :, element)
        first_spread = maximum(x_first) - minimum(x_first)
        second_spread = maximum(x_second) - minimum(x_second)
        if first_spread >= second_spread
            x_nodes = collect(x_first)
            y_nodes = collect(view(y_blocks, 1, :, element))
            z_block = Array(view(z_blocks, :, :, element))
        else
            x_nodes = collect(x_second)
            y_nodes = collect(view(y_blocks, :, 1, element))
            z_block = permutedims(Array(view(z_blocks, :, :, element)))
        end

        x_order = sortperm(x_nodes)
        y_order = sortperm(y_nodes)
        x_nodes = x_nodes[x_order]
        y_nodes = y_nodes[y_order]
        z_block = z_block[x_order, y_order]

        padding = 1.0e-12
        i_first = max(1, searchsortedfirst(x_grid, x_min - padding))
        i_last = min(nx, searchsortedlast(x_grid, x_max + padding))
        j_first = max(1, searchsortedfirst(y_grid, y_min - padding))
        j_last = min(ny, searchsortedlast(y_grid, y_max + padding))

        for j in j_first:j_last
            y_left, y_right, wy = rti_linear_bracket(y_nodes, y_grid[j])
            for i in i_first:i_last
                filled[j, i] && continue
                x_left, x_right, wx =
                    rti_linear_bracket(x_nodes, x_grid[i])
                z00 = z_block[x_left, y_left]
                z10 = z_block[x_right, y_left]
                z01 = z_block[x_left, y_right]
                z11 = z_block[x_right, y_right]
                lower = (1.0 - wx) * z00 + wx * z10
                upper = (1.0 - wx) * z01 + wx * z11
                grid[j, i] = (1.0 - wy) * lower + wy * upper
                filled[j, i] = true
            end
        end
    end

    missing_count = count(.!filled)
    missing_count == 0 ||
        error("Uniform DG sampling left $missing_count grid points uncovered")
    all(isfinite, grid) ||
        error("Uniform DG sampling produced non-finite values")
    return x_grid, y_grid, grid
end

# Parity-aware reflection residual on the complete cell-centered grid:
# even: ||q - Mq||_2 / ||q||_2
# odd:  ||q + Mq||_2 / ||q||_2
function rti_mirror_residual(grid; parity=:even, norm_epsilon=1.0e-14)
    ny, nx = size(grid)
    iseven(nx) || throw(ArgumentError("Mirror pairing requires an even Nx"))
    parity in (:even, :odd) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("parity must be :even or :odd"))

    numerator = 0.0
    denominator = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:div(nx, 2)
        q_left = grid[j, i]
        q_right = grid[j, nx + 1 - i]
        deviation = parity === :odd ?
                    q_left + q_right : q_left - q_right
        numerator += 2.0 * deviation^2
        denominator += q_left^2 + q_right^2
    end

    absolute_residual = sqrt(numerator)
    field_norm = sqrt(denominator)
    # In particular, rho*u is identically zero at t=0.
    if field_norm < norm_epsilon
        return absolute_residual
    end
    return absolute_residual / field_norm
end

function rti_save_symmetry_residuals!(state, data, t)
    sampling_options = (nx=rti_comparison_nx,
                        ny=rti_comparison_ny,
                        degree=state.polydeg,
                        cell_centered=true)
    rho_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho"; sampling_options...)
    rho_u_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_u";
                                           sampling_options...)
    rho_v_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_v";
                                           sampling_options...)
    rho_e_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_e";
                                           sampling_options...)

    residuals = (
        rho=rti_mirror_residual(rho_sample[3]; parity=:even),
        rho_u=rti_mirror_residual(rho_u_sample[3]; parity=:odd),
        rho_v=rti_mirror_residual(rho_v_sample[3]; parity=:even),
        rho_e=rti_mirror_residual(rho_e_sample[3]; parity=:even)
    )
    state.symmetry_residuals[t] = residuals
    return residuals
end

function rti_save_npz_file(integrator, state)
    semi_local = integrator.p
    t = integrator.t

    pd_cons = PlotData2D(integrator.u, semi_local;
                         solution_variables=cons2cons)
    # pd.data is a vector of complete variable arrays. Broadcasting getindex
    # over it would instead select one scalar from each variable.
    rho = pd_cons.data[1]
    rho_u = pd_cons.data[2]
    rho_v = pd_cons.data[3]
    rho_e = pd_cons.data[4]

    pd_prim = PlotData2D(integrator.u, semi_local;
                         solution_variables=cons2prim)
    p = pd_prim.data[4]
    y = vec(pd_cons.y)
    v_vec = vec(pd_prim.data[3])

    mass = rti_total_mass(integrator.u, semi_local)
    if t == 0.0 || !isfinite(state.initial_mass)
        state.initial_mass = mass
    end
    if t == 0.0 && !isfinite(state.initialization_dt)
        state.initialization_t = t
        state.initialization_dt = integrator.dt
    end

    mass_drift = mass - state.initial_mass
    relative_mass_drift = mass_drift / state.initial_mass
    rho_min, rho_max = extrema(rho)
    p_min, p_max = extrema(p)
    wall_bottom = rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v_vec, 0.0)
    wall_top = rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v_vec, 1.0)
    positivity_pass = rho_min > 0.0 && p_min > 0.0

    dt_last = NaN
    if !isempty(state.accepted_steps)
        dt_last = state.accepted_steps[end].dt
    end

    snapshot_data = Dict{String,Any}(
        "t" => t,
        "polydeg" => state.polydeg,
        "x" => pd_cons.x,
        "y" => pd_cons.y,
        "rho" => rho,
        "rho_u" => rho_u,
        "rho_v" => rho_v,
        "rho_e" => rho_e,
        "p" => p
    )
    filename = joinpath(state.npz_dir,
                        Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", t))
    npzwrite(filename, snapshot_data)
    state.snapshot_files[t] = filename

    # Use the complete archived state for the required diagnostic at every
    # save event, not only at the five post-processing times.
    symmetry = rti_save_symmetry_residuals!(state, snapshot_data, t)

    state.final_diagnostics = (
        t=t,
        mass=mass,
        mass_drift=mass_drift,
        relative_mass_drift=abs(relative_mass_drift),
        rho_min=rho_min,
        rho_max=rho_max,
        p_min=p_min,
        p_max=p_max,
        dt_last=dt_last,
        initialization_dt=state.initialization_dt,
        accepted_step_count=length(state.accepted_steps),
        max_abs_v_y0=wall_bottom,
        max_abs_v_y1=wall_top
    )
    if state === rti_default_diagnostics
        rti_final_diagnostics[] = state.final_diagnostics
    end

    println("Saved: $(state.label)/$(basename(filename))")
    Printf.@printf("Diagnostics t=%.5f: mass=%.16e, mass_drift=%.3e, relative_mass_drift=%.3e\n",
                   t, mass, mass_drift, relative_mass_drift)
    Printf.@printf("  rho_min=%.6e, rho_max=%.6e, p_min=%.6e, p_max=%.6e\n",
                   rho_min, rho_max, p_min, p_max)
    println("  positivity of rho/p: ", positivity_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
    Printf.@printf("  accepted_steps=%d, dt_last=%.3e, max_abs_v_y0=%.3e, max_abs_v_y1=%.3e\n",
                   length(state.accepted_steps), dt_last,
                   wall_bottom, wall_top)
    Printf.@printf("  parity-aware symmetry: R_rho=%.16e, R_rho_u=%.16e, R_rho_v=%.16e, R_rho_E=%.16e, max=%.16e\n",
                   symmetry.rho, symmetry.rho_u, symmetry.rho_v,
                   symmetry.rho_e,
                   max(symmetry.rho, symmetry.rho_u,
                       symmetry.rho_v, symmetry.rho_e))
    return nothing
end
```

```julia
# ## Methodology: Uniform Cartesian Sampling of DG Data
#
# Shared bracketing, element-block, grid-axis, uniform-sampling, and
# parity-aware residual routines are defined in Cell 8 so that they are
# available during every save callback. This cell retains the structural and
# post-processing diagnostics built on those routines.

# Maximum mirrored deviation, used by the t=0 renderer test.
function rti_max_mirrored_deviation(grid)
    ny, nx = size(grid)
    iseven(nx) || throw(ArgumentError("Mirror pairing requires an even Nx"))
    maximum_deviation = 0.0
    for j in 1:ny, i in 1:div(nx, 2)
        maximum_deviation = max(maximum_deviation,
                                abs(grid[j, i] - grid[j, nx + 1 - i]))
    end
    return maximum_deviation
end

# Backward-compatible name; the corrected test uses exact mirror pairs.
rti_max_x_deviation(grid) = rti_max_mirrored_deviation(grid)
```

- New Code (lines 310-356):
```julia
# Fill missing residuals for archive-only runs without overwriting the
# complete per-save history recorded by rti_save_npz_file. Comparison samples
# are still generated at the two requested comparison times.
function rti_compute_stage_diagnostic_data!(run;
                                            times=rti_symmetry_times,
                                            store_comparison=true)
    state = run.diagnostics
    for target_time in times
        residuals_available =
            haskey(state.symmetry_residuals, target_time)
        needs_comparison = store_comparison &&
                           any(t -> isapprox(target_time, t;
                                             rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-10),
                               rti_comparison_times)

        # Fresh runs already computed these residuals in their save callback.
        if residuals_available && !needs_comparison
            continue
        end

        filename = rti_snapshot_file(state, target_time)
        data = npzread(filename)
        sampling_options = (nx=rti_comparison_nx,
                            ny=rti_comparison_ny,
                            degree=run.polydeg,
                            cell_centered=true)

        rho_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho";
                                             sampling_options...)
        x_grid, y_grid, rho_grid = rho_sample
        nx = length(x_grid)
        for i in 1:nx
            isapprox(x_grid[i] + x_grid[nx + 1 - i], 0.25;
                     rtol=0.0, atol=8.0 * eps(Float64)) ||
                error("Cell-centered mirror grid pairing is not exact")
        end

        if !residuals_available
            rho_u_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_u";
                                                   sampling_options...)
            rho_v_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_v";
                                                   sampling_options...)
            rho_e_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "rho_e";
                                                   sampling_options...)
            residuals = (
                rho=rti_mirror_residual(rho_grid; parity=:even),
                rho_u=rti_mirror_residual(rho_u_sample[3]; parity=:odd),
                rho_v=rti_mirror_residual(rho_v_sample[3]; parity=:even),
                rho_e=rti_mirror_residual(rho_e_sample[3]; parity=:even)
            )
            state.symmetry_residuals[target_time] = residuals
        end

        if needs_comparison
            p_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(data, "p";
                                               sampling_options...)
            rti_store_comparison_sample!(run, target_time,
                                         x_grid, y_grid, rho_grid,
                                         p_sample[3])
        end
    end
    return state
end
```

Inspector summary:
 Implements a 2D DGSEM (Lobatto-Legendre) solver for the compressible Euler equations with gravity using Trixi.jl. Stage B is executed with polydeg=6, HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing (volume_flux_dg=flux_ranocha, volume_flux_fv=flux_lax_friedrichs), density-based Löhner AMR (base_level=4, med_level=5 @ 0.1, max_level=6 @ 0.3, interval=20, adapt_initial_condition=true), RDPK3SpFSAL49 with adaptive=false and CFL=0.30, and tspan=(0.0,3.0). The initial conditions match the prescribed Rayleigh-Taylor profiles exactly; boundaries are periodic in x and slip walls in y; the gravity source is (0,0,rho*g,rho*g*v). The code includes event-time de-duplication, roundoff-safe event snapping, a timestep audit regression gate for zero non-final accepted steps with dt<1e-8, baseline comparison against the required metrics, diagnostics at every save event, and a 2x4 rho/p summary plot.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

```julia
function rti_snapshot_file(state, target_time)
    for (t, filename) in state.snapshot_files
        if isapprox(t, target_time; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-10)
            return filename
        end
    end
    error("Missing snapshot for $(state.label) at t = $target_time")
end
```
