Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Cell 8: Data I/O & Surrogate Interoperability
- Action: Fix the fatal `BoundsError` by extracting complete nodal conservative fields instead of selecting individual `SVector` states. Support both `PlotData2D` storage layouts, then compute pressure and vertical velocity from those complete arrays.

- New Code (lines 163-171):
```julia
function rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v, wall_y)
    y_vec = vec(y)
    v_vec = vec(v)
    length(y_vec) == length(v_vec) ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("Wall coordinates and vertical velocity must have matching lengths"))
    isempty(y_vec) &&
        throw(ArgumentError("Wall diagnostic received empty coordinate data"))

    distances = abs.(y_vec .- wall_y)
    tolerance = 1.0e-10
    mask = distances .<= tolerance
    # Retain a fallback for floating-point variations in boundary coordinates.
    if !any(mask)
        mask = distances .<= minimum(distances) + tolerance
    end
    return maximum(abs.(v_vec[mask]))
end
```
- New Code (lines 349-364):
```julia
# Return complete variable arrays for either PlotData2D representation:
# a vector of variable arrays, or one conservative SVector per plot node.
function rti_complete_plot_fields(pd, variable_count)
    field_shape = size(pd.y)
    size(pd.x) == field_shape ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("PlotData2D x/y coordinate shapes do not match"))
    field_length = prod(field_shape)
    data = pd.data

    if data isa AbstractVector && length(data) == variable_count &&
       all(field -> field isa AbstractArray && length(field) == field_length,
           data)
        return [reshape(data[i], field_shape) for i in 1:variable_count]
    end

    length(data) == field_length ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("PlotData2D data and coordinates have incompatible sizes"))
    return [reshape(map(state -> state[i], data), field_shape)
            for i in 1:variable_count]
end

function rti_save_npz_file(integrator, state)
    semi_local = integrator.p
    t = integrator.t
    pd_cons = PlotData2D(integrator.u, semi_local;
                         solution_variables=cons2cons)
    rho, rho_u, rho_v, rho_e = rti_complete_plot_fields(pd_cons, 4)

    # Recover primitive fields from complete conservative nodal arrays.
    # This avoids selecting one four-component state from pd_prim.data.
    p = (equations.gamma - 1) .*
        (rho_e .- 0.5 .* (rho_u.^2 .+ rho_v.^2) ./ rho)
    y = vec(pd_cons.y)
    v_vec = vec(rho_v ./ rho)
    length(v_vec) == length(y) ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("Vertical velocity and y coordinates must have matching lengths"))
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

- New Code (lines 163-180):
```julia
function rti_wall_max_abs_v(y, v, wall_y)
    y_vec = vec(y)
    v_vec = vec(v)
    length(y_vec) == length(v_vec) ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("Wall coordinates and vertical velocity must have matching lengths"))
    isempty(y_vec) &&
        throw(ArgumentError("Wall diagnostic received empty coordinate data"))

    distances = abs.(y_vec .- wall_y)
    tolerance = 1.0e-10
    mask = distances .<= tolerance
    # Retain a fallback for floating-point variations in boundary coordinates.
    if !any(mask)
        mask = distances .<= minimum(distances) + tolerance
    end
    return maximum(abs.(v_vec[mask]))
end
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Cell 8: Data I/O & Surrogate Interoperability
- Action: The event vector is de-duplicated, but `PresetTimeCallback` independently registers its times as `tstops`. Together with the other output callback and the explicit `tstops` in `solve`, this creates multiple owners of the same schedule and can recreate ULP-sized steps. Use condition-only `DiscreteCallback`s while retaining the shared canonical schedule.
- Action: Preserve the initial NPZ save and advance an independent event cursor so every output event fires exactly once. The raw accepted-step recorder and regression audit remain unchanged.
- New Code (lines 462-471):
```julia
# Condition-only scheduled callback: rti_solve_stage owns the canonical
# tstops. PresetTimeCallback here would register the same events again.
function rti_build_scheduled_event_callback(events, affect!;
                                            initial_affect=false)
    canonical_events = rti_canonical_event_subset(
        events, rti_scheduled_event_times)
    next_event = Ref(1)

    function condition(u, t, integrator)
        index = next_event[]
        index <= length(canonical_events) || return false
        event = canonical_events[index]
        return t == event || rti_event_reached(t, event)
    end

    function scheduled_affect!(integrator)
        index = next_event[]
        index <= length(canonical_events) || return nothing
        affect!(integrator)
        next_event[] = index + 1
        return nothing
    end

    initialize = function (callback, u, t, integrator)
        next_event[] = 1
        if initial_affect && !isempty(canonical_events)
            event = canonical_events[1]
            if t == event || rti_event_reached(t, event)
                scheduled_affect!(integrator)
            end
        end
        return nothing
    end

    return DiscreteCallback(condition, scheduled_affect!;
                            initialize=initialize,
                            save_positions=(false, false))
end

function rti_build_save_callback(state)
    return rti_build_scheduled_event_callback(
        save_times,
        integrator -> rti_save_npz_file(integrator, state);
        initial_affect=true)
end

function rti_build_io_callbacks(state; amr_enabled=true)
    return CallbackSet(rti_build_dt_callback(state;
                                             amr_enabled=amr_enabled),
                       rti_build_save_callback(state))
end
```

- New Code (lines 55-70):
```julia
function rti_solve_stage(ode_stage, stage_callbacks)
    scheduled_callbacks =
        rti_add_roundoff_event_scheduling(stage_callbacks)
    event_tstops = filter(t -> t > tspan[1],
                          rti_scheduled_event_times)

    solution = solve(ode_stage, RDPK3SpFSAL49();
                     dt=1.0,
                     adaptive=false,
                     callback=scheduled_callbacks,
                     # Scheduled NPZ/Trixi callbacks provide every required
                     # full-state output. Do not register another saveat
                     # schedule alongside the canonical event tstops.
                     save_everystep=false,
                     save_start=false,
                     save_end=true,
                     tstops=event_tstops,
                     maxiters=1e7)

    isempty(solution.t) &&
        error("Time integration returned no saved times")
    isapprox(solution.t[end], tspan[2]; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-12) ||
        error("Time integration stopped at t = $(solution.t[end]), expected $(tspan[2])")
    return solution
end
```

```julia
            ("min_rho", current.rho_min, 0.192),

            ("max_abs_v_y0", current.max_abs_v_y0, 5.6e-5),
            ("max_abs_v_y1", current.max_abs_v_y1, 3.7e-4),
            ("t3_symmetry", current_symmetry, 1.1e-4)
        )
        println("  Approximate reference deviations (informational):")
        for (name, value, target) in references
            Printf.@printf("  %s: fresh=%.16e, approximate_reference=%.16e, |delta|=%.3e\n",
                           name, value, target, abs(value - target))
        end

        finite_pass = all(isfinite,
                          (current.t, current.mass, current.mass_drift,
                           current.relative_mass_drift,
                           current.rho_min, current.rho_max,
                           current.p_min, current.p_max,
                           current.max_abs_v_y0, current.max_abs_v_y1,
                           current_symmetry))
        final_time_pass = isapprox(current.t, tspan[2];
                                   rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-12)
        positivity_pass = current.rho_min > 0.0 && current.p_min > 0.0

        # This is a conservation sanity gate, not a loosened baseline
        # tolerance. The expected drift remains reported above.
        conservation_pass = isfinite(current.relative_mass_drift) &&
                            current.relative_mass_drift <= 1.0e-10

        # Wall values must remain small compared with the 0.025 velocity
        # perturbation and the order-one acoustic velocity scale.
        wall_pass = isfinite(current.max_abs_v_y0) &&
                    isfinite(current.max_abs_v_y1) &&
                    current.max_abs_v_y0 <= 1.0e-3 &&
                    current.max_abs_v_y1 <= 1.0e-3

        # Use the strategy's explicit fixed-mesh-control threshold.
        symmetry_pass = isfinite(current_symmetry) &&
                        current_symmetry <= 1.0e-2

        # Confirm that the archived final state contains a developed mixing
        # zone and resolved heavy/light spike-bubble structures, rather than
        # merely accepting any positive final density and pressure.
        final_sample = get(run.diagnostics.fixed_grid_samples, 3.0, nothing)
        structure_pass = final_sample !== nothing &&
                         final_sample.metrics.mixing_zone_count > 0 &&
                         final_sample.metrics.structural_count > 0

        # Validate every scheduled full-state output owned by this fresh run.
        snapshots_pass = true
        for target_time in save_times
            filename = nothing
            for (t, candidate) in run.diagnostics.snapshot_files
                if isapprox(t, target_time; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-10)
                    filename = candidate
                    break
                end
            end
            valid_snapshot = filename !== nothing && isfile(filename) &&
                             filesize(filename) > 0
            snapshots_pass = snapshots_pass && valid_snapshot
        end
        figure_pass = isfile(run.figure_file) &&
                      filesize(run.figure_file) > 0

        checks = (
            ("finite archived diagnostics", finite_pass),
            ("final time is t=3", final_time_pass),
            ("positivity of rho/p", positivity_pass),
            ("relative mass drift <= 1e-10", conservation_pass),
            ("wall max|v| <= 1e-3", wall_pass),
            ("t=3 parity-aware symmetry <= 1e-2", symmetry_pass),
            ("developed RTI mixing and spike/bubble structures",
             structure_pass),
            ("all scheduled full-state snapshots", snapshots_pass),
            ("rendered 2x4 Stage B figure", figure_pass)
        )
        for (name, passed) in checks
            println("  ", name, ": ", passed ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
        end

        comparison_pass = all(check -> check[2], checks)
        println("STAGE B PHYSICAL/OUTPUT VALIDATION (archive unavailable): ",
                comparison_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
        return comparison_pass
    end
```

Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

--- Strategy Report Received ---
# Scientific Strategy Report — Run 7 (Verification-Only Refinement)

**1. Problem Statement**

This is a **refinement iteration** of the single-mode compressible Rayleigh–Taylor Instability (RTI). We solve the 2D inviscid compressible Euler equations with a gravitational source ($g = +1.0$ in $y$) on $[0,0.25]\times[0,1]$, $t\in[0,3]$. The heavy fluid ($\rho=2$) occupies $y<0.5$, which lies "above" the light fluid ($\rho=1$) in the effective gravity frame (gravity points $+y$) — an unstable configuration with Atwood number $A = 1/3$ — seeded by a single-mode vertical velocity perturbation.

The prior run scored **99/100** and the Advisor confirmed **no physical, stability, or resolution defect**. The sole outstanding deficiency is an **incomplete verification/audit trail** (missing raw accepted-step audit output; skipped direct Stage B field comparison due to absent archives). Therefore this run is a **verification-only refinement**: identical physics, identical frozen Stage B discretization, with a completed timestep audit and archived baseline states.

**1. Mathematical Specification (Code-Ready Extraction)**

* **Governing Equations:** 2D Compressible Euler with gravitational source:
$$
\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\begin{pmatrix} \rho \\ \rho u \\ \rho v \\ \rho E \end{pmatrix}
+\frac{\partial}{\partial x}\begin{pmatrix} \rho u \\ \rho u^2 + p \\ \rho u v \\ u(\rho E + p) \end{pmatrix}
+\frac{\partial}{\partial y}\begin{pmatrix} \rho v \\ \rho u v \\ \rho v^2 + p \\ v(\rho E + p) \end{pmatrix}
=\begin{pmatrix} 0 \\ 0 \\ \rho g \\ \rho g v \end{pmatrix},
\qquad g = 1.0
$$
with equation of state and total energy:
$$
\rho E = \frac{p}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\rho\left(u^2 + v^2\right), \qquad \gamma = 1.4
$$

* **Domain:** $(x,y) \in [0,\, 0.25] \times [0,\, 1.0]$, $\quad t \in [0,\, 3.0]$

* **Boundary Conditions:**
  * $x$-direction ($x=0$, $x=0.25$): **Periodic**
  * $y$-direction ($y=0$, $y=1$): **Reflective / free-slip walls** (no-penetration $v=0$; mirror reflection of normal momentum)

* **Initial Conditions** (exact, every field):
$$
\rho(x,y) = 2.0 + (1.0 - 2.0)\cdot\frac{1}{2}\Big(1 + \tanh\big(50\,(y - 0.5)\big)\Big)
$$
$$
p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1, & y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5, & y \geq 0.5 \end{cases}
$$
$$
u(x,y) = 0
$$
$$
v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c(y) \cdot \cos(8\pi x)\cdot\big[\sin(\pi y)\big]^6,
\qquad c(y) = \sqrt{\frac{\gamma\, p(y)}{\rho(y)}}
$$
$$
\rho E(x,y) = \frac{p(y)}{\gamma - 1} + \frac{1}{2}\,\rho(y)\left(u^2 + v^2\right) = \frac{p(y)}{0.4} + \frac{1}{2}\,\rho(y)\,v(x,y)^2
$$

* **Constants & Parameters:**
  * $\gamma = 1.4$
  * Gravity vector $\mathbf{g} = [0,\, +1.0]^T$ (source adds momentum in $+y$; energy source $\rho g v$)
  * $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$, $\rho_{light} = 1.0$; interface center $y_i = 0.5$; smoothing steepness $50$
  * Perturbation amplitude $0.025$; mode $\cos(8\pi x)$ (single mode, wavelength $\lambda = 0.25$ = domain width)
  * $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$ (inviscid — no viscous/heat fluxes)
  * Atwood number $A = (\rho_h - \rho_l)/(\rho_h + \rho_l) = 1/3$
  * Hydrostatic consistency: $\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$ holds exactly in each half-domain ($2 = 2\cdot 1$ below, $1 = 1\cdot 1$ above); $p$ continuous at $y=0.5$ ($p=2.0$)

* **Expected Physics:** Effective gravity points $+y$; the heavy fluid is gravitationally "on top" → unstable. Spike descends toward $+y$ (upper wall), bubbles rise toward $-y$. Timeline (confirmed by prior runs): $t\approx0.75$ smooth single-mode interface displacement (linear growth); $t\approx1.5$ developed heavy-fluid spike with adjacent light-fluid bubbles; $t\approx2.25$ mushroom-head formation and vortex roll-up; $t=3.0$ secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz filaments, extensive mixing, wall interaction, possible weak compressive shocks (spike/wall interaction). Pressure remains predominantly smooth hydrostatic stratification ($p: 1 \to 2.5$).

**2. Strategic Methodology**

* **Can we obtain an exact solution? No.**
  * **Method of Characteristics:** Not feasible. MoC yields closed-form/implicit solutions only for 1D hyperbolic systems or simple-wave structures. This problem is genuinely multi-dimensional and nonlinear: the perturbed interface deposits baroclinic vorticity ($\nabla\rho \times \nabla p \neq 0$), undergoes vortex roll-up, and develops secondary KH instabilities and thin filaments. Characteristics intersect and entangle in 2D; no reducible implicit characteristic system exists past $t=0$. The only exact fact is the unperturbed hydrostatic equilibrium ($dp/dy = \rho g$), which serves as a $t=0$ consistency check, not a solution.
  * **Fourier Spectral:** Inappropriate — non-periodic in $y$ (slip walls) and the solution develops contact discontinuities and filaments → global Gibbs phenomenon.
  * **Separation of variables / Green's functions:** Not tractable for nonlinear multi-D evolution.
  * **Conclusion:** A numerical method is mandatory. The Advisor explicitly confirmed no architecture override is warranted.

* **Selection: Discontinuous Galerkin Spectral Element Method in 2D plus time.** It is the only toolbox method that simultaneously provides: (i) mixed periodic/wall boundary handling, (ii) hybrid DG–FV shock capturing for late-time compressive features, (iii) local AMR for the thin KH filaments, (iv) high-order accuracy in the smooth stratified bulk, and (v) robust treatment of the gravitational source on the hydrostatic background.

* **Run context:** The Advisor's diagnosis is definitive — *no physical stability or resolution defect*; the discretization is **frozen** (Stage B). This run changes **nothing** in the physics or numerics; it only completes the audit trail and archives the baseline.

**3. Implementation Requirements (CRITICAL)**

* **Configuration:** Use the **frozen Stage B configuration exactly** (Advisor-mandated retention — do NOT alter the discretization): `polydeg=6`, CFL $= 0.30$, HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing (DG volume flux `ranocha`, FV flux `lax_friedrichs`), density-based Löhner AMR with `ControllerThreeLevel` (base=4, med=5 @ 0.1, max=6 @ 0.3, interval=20, `adapt_initial_condition=true`), $t\in[0,3]$. Retain the existing time-integration configuration unchanged. No resolution changes are justified.

* **3.0.1 Adaptive Mesh Refinement & Shock Capturing — Variable Selection Analysis:**
  * **AMR sensor: Density ($\rho$).** *Signal-to-noise reasoning:* Every structural feature of interest in RTI — the material interface, descending spike, rising bubbles, mushroom roll-up, and KH filaments — **is** a density feature. Density remains sharp and localized at these features throughout the evolution (bounded $1 \to 2$), giving a high signal-to-noise ratio for refinement. Velocity, in contrast, becomes globally chaotic in the mixing zone at late times (vortex roll-up fills the domain with broad velocity gradients) — refining on it would trigger near-uniform refinement and waste resources. Pressure is dominated by the smooth hydrostatic background and under-tracks the interface. **Density is strictly superior for AMR on this PDE.**
  * **Shock-capturing sensor: Pressure ($p$).** The features requiring FV stabilization (late-time compression, spike–wall interaction, acoustic transients) are pressure-driven shocks. Using pressure avoids falsely flagging the density contact (a slip/contact discontinuity, not a shock), preserving high-order DG along the interface. This division of labor — density → AMR (structure tracking), pressure → shock capturing (compressive stabilization) — is retained per the Advisor.

* **3.1 Advisor-Mandated Adjustments (Overrides):**
  * **Verification-only refinement — do NOT alter the discretization.** The final validation block MUST print:
    1. Total accepted steps.
    2. Minimum non-final accepted $dt$.
    3. `count_nonfinal_dt_lt_1e-8` — must be **exactly zero**.
    4. Final-step $dt$, separately identified.
    5. An explicit **`TIMESTEP_AUDIT: PASS/FAIL`** line.
  * **Baseline archiving:** Archive the raw accepted-step times, the **full Stage B states** (all conservative variables at the scheduled snapshot times), and the rendered fields, so the next run can perform a **direct field comparison** rather than relying on approximate scalar references.
  * **Direct comparison if available:** If archived Stage B states from a prior run exist, load them and perform the direct rendered-field comparison (this was skipped last run due to absent archives).
  * Retain `polydeg=6`, CFL $=0.30$, HLLC, and the existing AMR/shock-capturing settings. Retain the existing diagnostic suite (mass drift, positivity $\min\rho,\min p$, wall $|v|$, symmetry residual) unchanged.

**4. Visualization Strategy**

* **Field Selection (Analysis):** Plot exactly **2 primary fields**:
  * **Density ($\rho$)** — the primary driver: RTI dynamics are entirely defined by the density contrast; interface, spike, bubbles, mushrooms, and filaments are all density structures.
  * **Pressure ($p$)** — secondary: captures the hydrostatic stratification and late-time compressible structure.
  * Do **not** plot $u, v, E$ (subplots become unreadable; velocity is noisy at late times). Do not compute derived quantities (no vorticity/Schlieren).
* **Task:** Plot $\rho$ (top row) and $p$ (bottom row) at **4 time steps**: $t = 0.75,\ 1.5,\ 2.25,\ 3.0$.
* **Layout:** Grid — Rows = Selected Fields ($\rho$, $p$), Columns = Time (2 × 4).
* **Saving Data:** Save the **full solution state** (all variables) at frequent intervals (`dt_save = 0.1`) for Ground Truth, and archive full conservative states at the scheduled snapshot times ($t = 0, 0.75, 1.5, 2.25, 3.0$) per the Advisor.
* **Constraints:**
  * Save as: `summary_all.png` (strictly).
  * **CRITICAL: DISABLE COLORBARS** (`colorbar=false`) — Julia.
  * Automatic per-panel color scaling; no fixed colormaps or clims.

```julia
# Archive-first verification closure. The canonical baseline is separate from
# the active production directory, so a fresh solve cannot overwrite the
# baseline before the direct comparison has completed.
rti_stage_b_baseline_root =
    joinpath(@__DIR__, "stage_b_polydeg6_baseline")
rti_stage_b_archive_roots = (
    rti_stage_b_baseline_root,
    joinpath(@__DIR__, "stage_b_polydeg6_event_fixed"),
    joinpath(@__DIR__, "stage_b_polydeg6")
)

# Copy the complete NPZ archive, including accepted-step history and rendered
# fields when present. Cell 12 also uses this helper to promote the fresh run
# to the canonical baseline only after rendering and baseline comparison.
function rti_copy_stage_b_archive(source_root, destination_root)
    source = abspath(source_root)
    destination = abspath(destination_root)
    source == destination && return destination

    source_npz = joinpath(source, "data_npz")
    isdir(source_npz) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Stage B archive has no data_npz directory: $source"))
    destination_npz = joinpath(destination, "data_npz")
    mkpath(destination_npz)

    for name in readdir(source_npz)
        source_file = joinpath(source_npz, name)
        isfile(source_file) || continue
        cp(source_file, joinpath(destination_npz, name); force=true)
    end

    source_figure = joinpath(source, "summary.png")
    if isfile(source_figure)
        cp(source_figure, joinpath(destination, "summary.png"); force=true)
    end
    return destination
end

function rti_load_archived_stage_b()
    degree = 6
    required_fields =
        ("polydeg", "x", "y", "rho", "rho_u", "rho_v", "rho_e", "p")

    for candidate_root in rti_stage_b_archive_roots
        candidate_npz = joinpath(candidate_root, "data_npz")
        isdir(candidate_npz) || continue

        candidate_snapshots = Dict{Float64,String}()
        valid = true
        for target_time in rti_symmetry_times
            filename = joinpath(
                candidate_npz,
                Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
            if !isfile(filename)
                valid = false
                break
            end

            data = npzread(filename)
            if !all(field -> haskey(data, field), required_fields)
                valid = false
                break
            end
            archived_degree = data["polydeg"]
            archived_degree = archived_degree isa Number ?
                              Int(archived_degree) : Int(only(archived_degree))
            if archived_degree != degree
                valid = false
                break
            end
            candidate_snapshots[target_time] = filename
        end
        valid || continue

        final_data = npzread(candidate_snapshots[3.0])
        state_dofs = rti_archived_stage_b_state_dofs(final_data, degree)

        # A legacy event_fixed archive must be copied now: the fresh Stage B
        # run below reuses that directory. Canonical archives are already
        # isolated and rti_copy_stage_b_archive returns them unchanged.
        archive_root = rti_copy_stage_b_archive(
            candidate_root, rti_stage_b_baseline_root)
        archive_npz = joinpath(archive_root, "data_npz")
        diagnostics = rti_new_stage_diagnostics(
            "stage_b_polydeg6_baseline", degree, archive_npz)
        for target_time in rti_symmetry_times
            diagnostics.snapshot_files[target_time] = joinpath(
                archive_npz, basename(candidate_snapshots[target_time]))
        end

        println("Loaded Stage B baseline archive from ", candidate_root)
        # final_diagnostics remains uninitialized here; Cell 12 recomputes
        # mass drift, positivity, and wall diagnostics from the archive.
        return (
            label="stage_b_polydeg6_baseline",
            polydeg=degree,
            cfl=0.30,
            semi=nothing,
            sol=nothing,
            diagnostics=diagnostics,
            npz_dir=archive_npz,
            trixi_dir=joinpath(archive_root, "trixi_output"),
            figure_file=joinpath(archive_root, "summary.png"),
            fixed_refinement_level=nothing,
            state_dofs=state_dofs
        )
    end
    return nothing
end

# Load the previous run solely as a baseline. Its isolated files are not
# overwritten by the fresh verification run.
rti_archived_stage_b = rti_load_archived_stage_b()
rti_stage_b_baseline = rti_archived_stage_b
if rti_stage_b_baseline === nothing
    println("Previous Stage B archive unavailable; the fresh run will be validated physically and archived for the next direct comparison")
else
    println("Retained previous Stage B archive for direct rendered-field comparison")
end
println("Running frozen Stage B verification: polydeg=6, CFL=0.30")
rti_stage_b = rti_run_independent_stage(
    "stage_b_polydeg6_event_fixed", 6, 0.30)
# This iteration has no resolution refinement or Stage A production run.
rti_resolution_runs = (rti_stage_b,)
```

New Code (lines 220-221):
```julia

# Rendered-field archives use exactly the renderer's default uniform grid,
# rather than the higher-resolution cell-centered symmetry diagnostic grid.
function rti_rendered_field_file(run, target_time)
    return joinpath(
        run.diagnostics.npz_dir,
        Printf.@sprintf("rendered_fields_time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
end

function rti_checked_rendered_sample(x, y, rho, p)
    x_grid = Float64.(collect(x))
    y_grid = Float64.(collect(y))
    rho_grid = Float64.(Array(rho))
    p_grid = Float64.(Array(p))

    ndims(x_grid) == 1 && ndims(y_grid) == 1 ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Rendered-field axes must be one-dimensional"))
    ndims(rho_grid) == 2 && ndims(p_grid) == 2 ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Rendered density and pressure must be matrices"))
    size(rho_grid) == (length(y_grid), length(x_grid)) ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("Rendered density and axes have incompatible sizes"))
    size(p_grid) == size(rho_grid) ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("Rendered density and pressure grids differ"))

    expected_x, expected_y =
        rti_grid_axes(length(x_grid), length(y_grid); cell_centered=false)
    x_grid == expected_x && y_grid == expected_y ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Rendered fields are not on the endpoint-inclusive renderer grid"))
    all(isfinite, x_grid) && all(isfinite, y_grid) &&
        all(isfinite, rho_grid) && all(isfinite, p_grid) ||
        throw(ArgumentError("Rendered-field archive contains non-finite values"))

    return (x=x_grid, y=y_grid, rho=rho_grid, p=p_grid)
end

# Called by Cell 12 with the same sampled_snapshots used to construct the
# summary panels, ensuring that the archived fields are the rendered fields.
function rti_archive_rendered_fields!(run, sampled_snapshots)
    length(sampled_snapshots) == length(visualization_times) ||
        throw(DimensionMismatch("One rendered sample is required per visualization time"))
    mkpath(run.diagnostics.npz_dir)
    files = Dict{Float64,String}()

    for (target_time, sample) in
        zip(visualization_times, sampled_snapshots)
        checked = rti_checked_rendered_sample(
            sample.x, sample.y, sample.rho, sample.p)
        filename = rti_rendered_field_file(run, target_time)
        npzwrite(filename, Dict{String,Any}(
            "t" => target_time,
            "polydeg" => run.polydeg,
            "x" => checked.x,
            "y" => checked.y,
            "rho" => checked.rho,
            "p" => checked.p,
            "nx" => length(checked.x),
            "ny" => length(checked.y)
        ))
        files[target_time] = filename
    end
    return files
end

function rti_read_rendered_fields(run, target_time)
    filename = rti_rendered_field_file(run, target_time)
    isfile(filename) || return nothing

    data = npzread(filename)
    required = ("t", "polydeg", "x", "y", "rho", "p")
    all(field -> haskey(data, field), required) ||
        error("Incomplete rendered-field archive: $filename")

    archived_t = data["t"]
    archived_t = archived_t isa Number ?
                 Float64(archived_t) : Float64(only(archived_t))
    isapprox(archived_t, target_time; rtol=0.0, atol=1.0e-10) ||
        error("Rendered-field archive time mismatch: $filename")

    archived_degree = data["polydeg"]
    archived_degree = archived_degree isa Number ?
                      Int(archived_degree) : Int(only(archived_degree))
    archived_degree == run.polydeg ||
        error("Rendered-field archive polynomial degree mismatch: $filename")

    return rti_checked_rendered_sample(
        data["x"], data["y"], data["rho"], data["p"])
end

# Reconstruct legacy rendered fields from complete conservative snapshots.
# This is also the common sampling path used by the fresh-run renderer.
function rti_sample_rendered_fields(run, target_time)
    data = npzread(rti_snapshot_file(run.diagnostics, target_time))
    rho_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(
        data, "rho"; degree=run.polydeg)
    p_sample = rti_sample_uniform_grid(
        data, "p"; degree=run.polydeg)
    rho_sample[1] == p_sample[1] && rho_sample[2] == p_sample[2] ||
        error("Density and pressure renderer grids differ")
    return rti_checked_rendered_sample(
        rho_sample[1], rho_sample[2], rho_sample[3], p_sample[3])
end

# Prefer the persisted rendered arrays. Older full-state archives remain
# usable for direct comparison by regenerating those arrays deterministically.
function rti_rendered_fields_for_comparison(run, target_time)
    archived = rti_read_rendered_fields(run, target_time)
    archived !== nothing && return archived
    return rti_sample_rendered_fields(run, target_time)
end

function rti_rendered_field_archive_complete(run)
    return all(target_time ->
               isfile(rti_rendered_field_file(run, target_time)),
               visualization_times)
end
```

```julia
    sampled_snapshots = Any[]
    for target_time in visualization_times

              rti_sample_rendered_fields(run, target_time))
```

New Code (lines 538-564):
```julia
    # Compare exactly the endpoint-inclusive fields used by the renderer.
    # Legacy baselines without rendered-field files are reconstructed from
    # their complete conservative states through the same sampling path.
    field_times = Float64[]
    field_variable_ids = Int[]
    field_relative_l2 = Float64[]
    field_max_abs_delta = Float64[]
    field_passes = Bool[]
    relative_l2_tolerance = 1.0e-8

    for target_time in visualization_times
        fresh_sample =
            rti_rendered_fields_for_comparison(run, target_time)
        baseline_sample =
            rti_rendered_fields_for_comparison(baseline, target_time)

        grids_match = fresh_sample.x == baseline_sample.x &&
                      fresh_sample.y == baseline_sample.y &&
                      size(fresh_sample.rho) == size(baseline_sample.rho) &&
                      size(fresh_sample.p) == size(baseline_sample.p)
        if !grids_match
            comparison_pass = false
            println("  rendered fields at t=$target_time: FAIL " *
                    "(renderer grids differ)")
            continue
        end

        for variable in ("rho", "p")
            fresh_grid = variable == "rho" ?
                         fresh_sample.rho : fresh_sample.p
            baseline_grid = variable == "rho" ?
                            baseline_sample.rho : baseline_sample.p

            difference_grid = fresh_grid .- baseline_grid
            max_abs_delta = maximum(abs.(difference_grid))
            difference_l2 = sqrt(sum(abs2, difference_grid))
            baseline_l2 = sqrt(sum(abs2, baseline_grid))
            relative_l2 = baseline_l2 > 0.0 ?
                          difference_l2 / baseline_l2 :
                          (difference_l2 == 0.0 ? 0.0 : Inf)

            field_pass = isfinite(relative_l2) &&
                         isfinite(max_abs_delta) &&
                         relative_l2 <= relative_l2_tolerance
            comparison_pass = comparison_pass && field_pass

            push!(field_times, target_time)
            push!(field_variable_ids, variable == "rho" ? 1 : 2)
            push!(field_relative_l2, relative_l2)
            push!(field_max_abs_delta, max_abs_delta)
            push!(field_passes, field_pass)

            Printf.@printf("  rendered %s, t=%.2f: relative_L2=%.16e, max_abs_delta=%.3e, tolerance=%.3e, %s\n",
                           variable, target_time, relative_l2,
                           max_abs_delta, relative_l2_tolerance,
                           field_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
        end
    end

    direct_fields_pass =
        length(field_passes) == 2 * length(visualization_times) &&
        all(field_passes)
    comparison_pass = comparison_pass && direct_fields_pass

    npzwrite(joinpath(run.diagnostics.npz_dir,
                      "rendered_field_comparison.npz"),
             Dict{String,Any}(
                 "t" => field_times,
                 # variable_id=1 denotes density; variable_id=2 denotes pressure.
                 "variable_id" => field_variable_ids,
                 "relative_l2" => field_relative_l2,
                 "max_abs_delta" => field_max_abs_delta,
                 "pass" => field_passes,
                 "relative_l2_tolerance" => relative_l2_tolerance
             ))
    println("STAGE B DIRECT RENDERED-FIELD COMPARISON: ",
            direct_fields_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
```

```julia
function rti_timestep_audit_verified(audit)
    return audit.regression_pass &&
           audit.nonfinal_tiny_count == 0 &&
           isfinite(audit.ordinary_min) &&
           audit.ordinary_min > 0.0 &&
           isfinite(audit.final_dt) &&
           audit.final_dt > 0.0 &&
           audit.final_at_tspan
end

function rti_archive_verified_stage_b_baseline(run)
    state = run.diagnostics
    history_file = joinpath(state.npz_dir, "accepted_step_history.npz")
    isfile(history_file) && filesize(history_file) > 0 ||
        error("Raw accepted-step history is missing for $(run.label)")

    history = npzread(history_file)
    required_history = ("t", "tprev", "dt", "iter", "amr_event")
    all(key -> haskey(history, key), required_history) ||
        error("Accepted-step history is incomplete for $(run.label)")
    length(history["t"]) == length(state.accepted_steps) ||
        error("Accepted-step history does not match the raw in-memory history")

    # Verify every frequent full-state output before promoting the archive.
    for target_time in save_times
        filename = rti_snapshot_file(state, target_time)
        isfile(filename) && filesize(filename) > 0 ||
            error("Missing full-state snapshot at t=$target_time")
    end
    rti_rendered_field_archive_complete(run) ||
        error("The fresh rendered density/pressure archive is incomplete")

    source_root = joinpath(@__DIR__, run.label)
    rti_copy_stage_b_archive(source_root, rti_stage_b_baseline_root)

    # Verify the isolated handoff used by the next run's Cell 10 loader.
    canonical_npz = joinpath(rti_stage_b_baseline_root, "data_npz")
    isfile(joinpath(canonical_npz, "accepted_step_history.npz")) ||
        error("Canonical baseline is missing its accepted-step history")
    for target_time in rti_symmetry_times
        filename = joinpath(
            canonical_npz,
            Printf.@sprintf("time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
        isfile(filename) && filesize(filename) > 0 ||
            error("Canonical baseline is missing the state at t=$target_time")
    end
    for target_time in visualization_times
        filename = joinpath(
            canonical_npz,
            Printf.@sprintf("rendered_fields_time_%08.5f.npz", target_time))
        isfile(filename) && filesize(filename) > 0 ||
            error("Canonical baseline is missing rendered fields at t=$target_time")
    end

    println("STAGE B BASELINE ARCHIVE: ", rti_stage_b_baseline_root)
    println("  archived raw accepted-step times/history")
    println("  archived full-state snapshots: ", length(save_times))
    println("  archived rendered rho/p field sets: ",
            length(visualization_times))
    return rti_stage_b_baseline_root
end

function rti_print_final_timestep_audit(run)
    audit = rti_timestep_audit(run.diagnostics; final_time=tspan[2])
    audit_pass = rti_timestep_audit_verified(audit)

    println("\nFINAL VALIDATION BLOCK: RAW ACCEPTED-STEP AUDIT")
    Printf.@printf("Total accepted steps: %d\n",
                   audit.accepted_step_count)
    Printf.@printf("Minimum non-final accepted dt: %.16e\n",
                   audit.ordinary_min)
    Printf.@printf("count_nonfinal_dt_lt_1e-8: %d\n",
                   audit.nonfinal_tiny_count)
    Printf.@printf("Final-step dt: %.16e\n", audit.final_dt)
    Printf.@printf("Final-step t: %.16e\n", audit.final_t)
    println("Raw accepted-step history: ",
            joinpath(run.diagnostics.npz_dir,
                     "accepted_step_history.npz"))
    println("TIMESTEP_AUDIT: ", audit_pass ? "PASS" : "FAIL")
    return (audit=audit, pass=audit_pass)
end
```

Inspector summary:
 The code implements a numerical DGSEM solution of the 2D inviscid compressible Euler equations with gravitational source g=+1.0 in y on the domain [0,0.25]x[0,1] for t in [0,3]. It uses CompressibleEulerEquations2D with gamma=1.4 and implements the exact initial density tanh profile, piecewise hydrostatic pressure, zero u, and the specified single-mode vertical velocity perturbation; x boundaries are periodic and y boundaries are reflective free-slip walls. The frozen Stage B configuration is used: polydeg=6, CFL=0.30, HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann-Gassner shock capturing with ranocha DG volume flux and lax_friedrichs FV flux, and density-based Lohner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel base=4, med=5 at threshold 0.1, max=6 at threshold 0.3, interval=20, and adapt_initial_condition=true. The run spans t=0 to 3, saves full conservative states at dt_save=0.1 and at the scheduled visualization times, performs the advisor-mandated accepted-step timestep audit, archives accepted-step history/full states/rendered fields, performs a direct rendered-field comparison when a prior baseline exists, and renders a 2x4 summary_all.png of density and pressure at t=0.75,1.5,2.25,3.0 with colorbars disabled. All required strategy components are present.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

Inspector summary:
 The implementation is a numerical 2D DGSEM solver for the inviscid compressible Euler equations with γ=1.4, solved on [0,0.25]×[0,1] over t∈[0,3]. Stage B uses polydeg=6, CFL=0.30, HLLC surface flux, pressure-based Hennemann–Gassner shock capturing with DG volume flux_ranocha and FV flux_lax_friedrichs, and density-based Löhner AMR with ControllerThreeLevel base=4, med=5 at 0.1, max=6 at 0.3, interval=20, adapt_initial_condition=true. Time integration uses RDPK3SpFSAL49 with adaptive=false and StepsizeCallback. The initial conditions exactly match the strategy: smoothed density interface, piecewise hydrostatic pressure, zero u, single-mode vertical velocity perturbation with c(y)=sqrt(γp/ρ), and prim2cons energy. Gravity source is (0,0,ρg,ρgv) with g=+1.0. x-boundaries are periodic and y-boundaries are slip walls. The code includes the advisor-mandated accepted-step audit, raw accepted-step history archiving, full conservative state snapshots at 0.1 intervals plus scheduled snapshot times, rendered ρ/p field archiving, baseline comparison when archives exist, and a 2×4 summary_all.png figure with colorbar disabled.
Proposed name:CompressibleEuler_RayleighTaylor_Periodic_Reflective\Question :Let's solve the 2D Compressible Euler equations for the Rayleigh-Taylor Instability.

**Physics Parameters:**
* **Equations:** Compressible Euler ($\gamma = 1.4$).
* **Gravity:** $g = 1.0$ acting in the **positive $y$-direction** (The source term adds momentum in $+y$).
* **Inviscid:** $\mu = 0$, thermal conductivity $k = 0$.

**Domain:**
$(x,y) \in [0, 0.25] \times [0, 1.0]$
Time interval: $t \in [0, 3.0]$

**Boundary Conditions:**
* $x$-direction: **Periodic**.
* $y$-direction: **Reflective / Free-slip walls** at $y=0$ and $y=1$ (No-penetration condition $v=0$).

**Initial Conditions:**
The domain is split at the interface $y=0.5$.
* **Density ($\rho$):** A smoothed interface between $\rho_{heavy} = 2.0$ (bottom) and $\rho_{light} = 1.0$ (top).
    $$ \rho(y) = \rho_{heavy} + (\rho_{light} - \rho_{heavy}) \cdot \frac{1}{2}\left(1 + \tanh\left(50(y - 0.5)\right)\right) $$
* **Pressure ($p$):** Hydrostatic balance ($\frac{dp}{dy} = \rho g$).
    $$ p(y) = \begin{cases} 2y + 1 & \text{if } y < 0.5 \\ y + 1.5 & \text{if } y \geq 0.5 \end{cases} $$
* **Velocity:** Initialized with zero horizontal velocity ($u=0$) and a perturbation in vertical velocity ($v$):
    $$ v(x,y) = -0.025 \cdot c \cdot \cos(8\pi x) \cdot \sin(\pi y)^6 $$
    Where $c = \sqrt{\gamma p / \rho}$ is the local speed of sound.

## Section 3: Forensic Analysis & Feedback

### Observation

**Qualitative plot assessment:**

- At \(t=0.75\), the density interface shows a smooth, single-mode displacement while pressure remains predominantly hydrostatically stratified.
- At \(t=1.5\), a coherent heavy-fluid spike and adjacent bubble structure have developed.
- At \(t=2.25\), the spike forms a mushroom-shaped head with visible vortex roll-up.
- At \(t=3.0\), secondary Kelvin–Helmholtz-like filaments and extensive mixing are visible, together with interaction near the upper wall. These small-scale structures remain well defined; there is no obvious domain-wide ringing, blank panel, or non-physical blow-up.
- Pressure remains mostly stratified, with localized late-time variations consistent with compressible motion and mixing. Its mottled late-time appearance is not, by itself, evidence of numerical oscillation.

**Log assessment:**

- The run reached \(t=3\) and ended with `Done.`; both `TIMESTEP_AUDIT` and `STAGE B VERIFICATION` report **PASS**.
- The raw accepted-step audit is complete:
  - Total accepted steps: **38,087**
  - Minimum non-final accepted \(dt\): **\(3.9336147810153932\times10^{-6}\)**
  - Non-final steps with \(dt<10^{-8}\): **0**
  - Final-step \(dt\): **\(2.8361614368588306\times10^{-5}\)**
- Relative mass drift is only **\(6.804631\times10^{-12}\)** in magnitude, indicating excellent discrete mass conservation.
- Positivity is maintained: \(\min\rho=0.3444687\) and \(\min p=1.207574\). The density reduction below its initial minimum is compatible with compressible expansion and is not a positivity failure.
- Wall-normal velocity maxima are small: \(5.69\times10^{-5}\) at \(y=0\) and \(2.10\times10^{-4}\) at \(y=1\).
- The largest corrected parity-aware symmetry residual is **\(1.386427\times10^{-3}\)**, below the \(10^{-2}\) threshold; the fixed-mesh symmetry contingency was therefore correctly not triggered.
- Thirty-three full-state snapshots and four rendered field sets were archived. A prior baseline was unavailable, so a direct baseline-to-baseline field comparison could not be performed; the conditional requirement was handled by archiving this run as the baseline.

### Error Check

The supplied stdout tail contains **no** line labeled `Minimum achieved error:`, so no such value can be extracted.

**Quantitative error is not applicable as no ground truth is available.** The mass drift and symmetry residual are conservation and symmetry diagnostics, not solution errors against an analytical reference. The unperturbed hydrostatic state provides only an initial-equilibrium check, not an exact solution of the nonlinear time-dependent RTI problem.

### Diagnosis

No remaining failure phenomenon is identified. The previous deficiency was an incomplete verification/audit trail rather than a physical or numerical defect; the present run supplies the raw timestep audit and baseline archives.

The frozen Stage B method remains methodologically appropriate:

- **HLLC** is suitable for Euler contact and shock structures.
- **Pressure-based shock capturing** targets compressive features without unnecessarily applying first-order stabilization to every density interface.
- **Density-based AMR** efficiently tracks spikes, bubbles, and filaments.
- **DGSEM with \(N=6\)** provides high-order accuracy in smooth regions, while the hybrid DG–FV formulation supplies robustness where needed.
- The observed 38,087 steps and small late-time steps are consistent with the cost of resolving the developing small-scale dynamics, not evidence of a timestep collapse.

An exact analytical method is not feasible for the full nonlinear, multidimensional evolution with baroclinic vorticity generation, mushroom roll-up, and secondary instabilities. Consequently, no numerical-to-exact architecture override is warranted.

### Targeted Improvements

- **For Integrity:** None.
- **For Accuracy:** None.
- **For Details:** None. No further mesh refinement or polynomial-degree increase is justified by the evidence.
- **For Optimality:** None.

**Recommendation:** Freeze the Stage B configuration and use the archived full conservative-state snapshots—not the rendered PNG—as the PINN ground-truth dataset. Further refinement iterations would be redundant.
